# Fases 1 e 2 (Specs 10 a 15) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implementar as specs 10 a 15 do OutPay: concorrencia de saldo, deposito simulado, idempotencia, auditoria, limites de operacao e tratamento padronizado de falhas.

**Architecture:** Toda operacao que altera saldo passa por duas camadas: um servico externo sem transacao (`TransferService`, `DepositService`) que repete a operacao com `balanceRetryTemplate` em conflito de versao, e um use case `@Transactional` que faz idempotencia, limites, movimentacao e auditoria de sucesso na mesma transacao. Falhas de negocio sao `BusinessException` com um `ErrorCode` do catalogo; o mesmo code vai para a resposta HTTP e para `audit_events`. Tabelas com SQL especifico do PostgreSQL (`idempotency_keys`, `audit_events`) sao acessadas com `JdbcClient`; entidades financeiras ganham historico com Hibernate Envers.

**Tech Stack:** Java 21 (alvo), Spring Boot 4.1.0, Spring Framework 7.0.8 (`org.springframework.core.retry`), Spring Security 7 (resource server JWT), Hibernate 7.4.1 + Envers, Jackson 3 (`tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper`), PostgreSQL 16, Flyway, JUnit 5, AssertJ, Testcontainers 2.0.5.

**Spec:** `docs/specs/10-balance-concurrency.md`, `docs/specs/11-deposit.md`, `docs/specs/12-idempotency.md`, `docs/specs/13-audit.md`, `docs/specs/14-operation-limits.md`, `docs/specs/15-failure-handling.md` (visao geral em `docs/core.md`).

## Decisoes de Implementacao

Pontos em que o plano detalha ou ajusta as specs. Todos precisam da aprovacao do usuario junto com o plano.

1. **Catalogo da spec 15:** os codes de token continuam `EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_INVALID|EXPIRED|ALREADY_USED` e `PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_INVALID|EXPIRED|ALREADY_USED`, contrato das specs 04 e 05. A spec 15 listava `INVALID_EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN` e `INVALID_PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN`, o que quebraria clientes e apagaria a diferenca entre expirado e ja usado. A Task 2 corrige a spec 15.
2. **`BusinessException`:** toda excecao de negocio passa a estender `common/error/BusinessException`, que carrega o `ErrorCode`. O `GlobalExceptionHandler` tem um unico handler para elas, e `TransferService`/`DepositService` usam o mesmo code em `audit_events.failure_code`.
3. **`JdbcClient` em vez de entidade JPA** para `idempotency_keys` e `audit_events`. Motivo: `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, `jsonb` e tabela append-only. O `JdbcClient` participa da mesma transacao do `JpaTransactionManager`.
4. **Relogio unico:** `TransactionEntity.completedTransfer/completedDeposit` recebem `Instant occurredAt` vindo do bean `Clock`. Sem isso, `created_at` e as janelas de limite da spec 14 usariam relogios diferentes.
5. **`Idempotency-Key` lido com `required = false`** e validado por `IdempotencyKeys.require`. Header ausente, vazio ou so com espacos vira 400 `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED`.
6. **`Money.parse` rejeita mais de 17 digitos inteiros** com 400 `INVALID_AMOUNT`, para nunca estourar `NUMERIC(19, 2)`.
7. **`OptimisticLockingFailureException` fora de operacao de saldo** (exemplo: dois `PUT /wallets/me/limits` simultaneos) vira 409 `DATA_CONFLICT`.
8. **Testes de concorrencia simulam o cliente**, que repete a chamada quando recebe 409 `BALANCE_CONFLICT`. So assim "exatamente 10 sucessos" (spec 10) e deterministico com 20 threads e 4 tentativas por chamada.
9. **Envers:** `hibernate.envers.do_not_audit_optimistic_locking_field=false`, senao o Envers omite `version` e `wallets_aud.version` (spec 13) nao existiria.
10. **`LimitsProperties`:** `timezone`, `night.start` e `night.end` sao `String` validadas no construtor do record, sem depender de conversores do binder.
11. **Ordem das tarefas:** a infraestrutura de testes e o catalogo de erros vem antes das features, para que deposito, idempotencia e limites ja nascam com `ErrorCode` e testes de integracao.

## Review Focus

1. Valor gigante como `"100000000000000000000"` em deposito ou transferencia deve dar 400 `INVALID_AMOUNT`, nunca 409 ou 500 por estouro de `NUMERIC(19, 2)`. Testes: `MoneyTest` (Task 4) e `DepositIntegrationTest` (Task 6).
2. `Idempotency-Key` vazio ou so com espacos deve dar 400 `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED`, nunca 500 nem uma chave `""` gravada. Teste: `IdempotencyIntegrationTest` (Task 7).
3. Com o servidor em UTC, dia, mes e janela noturna sao calculados em `America/Sao_Paulo`. Uma transferencia de 30/09 as 23:00 em Sao Paulo (01/10 02:00Z) nao conta no mes de outubro. Testes: `LimitPeriodsTest` e `LimitsIntegrationTest` (Task 10).
4. Transferencia para usuario inexistente deve dar 404 `WALLET_NOT_FOUND`, gerar `TRANSFER_REJECTED` e nao deixar chave idempotente gravada. Teste: `AuditIntegrationTest` (Task 8).
5. Conflito de versao fora de operacao de saldo, como `PUT /wallets/me/limits` concorrente, deve dar 409 `DATA_CONFLICT`, nao 500. Teste: `GlobalExceptionHandlerTest` (Task 11).

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestUser;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class WalletBalanceIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    @Test
    void shouldReadBalanceFromRealDatabase() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");

        mockMvc.perform(get("/wallets/me/balance").header("Authorization", bearer(user)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.walletId").value(user.walletId().toString()))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.balance").value("100.00"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.currency").value("BRL"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldStartEveryTestWithEmptyTables() {
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from users", Long.class)).isZero();
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from wallets", Long.class)).isZero();
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/support/AbstractIntegrationTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.AccessTokenService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.domain.UserEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.repository.UserRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import org.springframework.boot.webmvc.test.autoconfigure.AutoConfigureMockMvc;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;

@SpringBootTest
@AutoConfigureMockMvc
@Import(IntegrationTestConfiguration.class)
public abstract class AbstractIntegrationTest {

    protected static final String DEFAULT_PASSWORD = "Senha1234";

    @Autowired
    protected MockMvc mockMvc;

    @Autowired
    protected JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

    @Autowired
    protected TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    @Autowired
    private UserRepository userRepository;

    @Autowired
    private WalletRepository walletRepository;

    @Autowired
    private AccessTokenService accessTokenService;

    @Autowired
    private PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;

    @BeforeEach
    void cleanDatabase() {
        String tables = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
                select string_agg(quote_ident(tablename), ', ')
                from pg_tables
                where schemaname = 'public'
                  and tablename <> 'flyway_schema_history'
                """, String.class);
        jdbcTemplate.execute("TRUNCATE TABLE " + tables + " RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE");
    }

    protected TestUser createVerifiedUser(String name, String initialBalance) {
        return transactionTemplate.execute(status -> {
            UserEntity user = new UserEntity(
                    name,
                    UUID.randomUUID() + "@email.com",
                    passwordEncoder.encode(DEFAULT_PASSWORD),
                    randomDocument()
            );
            user.markEmailVerified();
            userRepository.save(user);

            WalletEntity wallet = new WalletEntity(user);
            BigDecimal balance = new BigDecimal(initialBalance);
            if (balance.signum() > 0) {
                wallet.credit(balance);
            }
            walletRepository.save(wallet);

            return new TestUser(
                    user.getId(),
                    wallet.getId(),
                    user.getEmail(),
                    accessTokenService.issue(user).accessToken()
            );
        });
    }

    protected String bearer(TestUser user) {
        return "Bearer " + user.accessToken();
    }

    protected BigDecimal balanceOf(TestUser user) {
        return jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select balance from wallets where id = ?",
                BigDecimal.class,
                user.walletId()
        );
    }

    protected BigDecimal totalBalance() {
        return jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select coalesce(sum(balance), 0) from wallets", BigDecimal.class);
    }

    private static String randomDocument() {
        return String.format("%011d", ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextLong(1L, 100_000_000_000L));
    }
}
```

`TRUNCATE` nao dispara triggers de linha, entao continua funcionando depois que `audit_events` ganhar o trigger append-only (Task 8). O documento aleatorio nao precisa ser CPF valido porque o usuario e criado direto pelo repositorio.

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/support/TestJwt.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import com.nimbusds.jose.JOSEException;
import com.nimbusds.jose.JWSAlgorithm;
import com.nimbusds.jose.JWSHeader;
import com.nimbusds.jose.crypto.MACSigner;
import com.nimbusds.jwt.JWTClaimsSet;
import com.nimbusds.jwt.SignedJWT;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.UUID;

public final class TestJwt {

    public static final String SECRET = "wallet-pay-test-secret-with-32-bytes";

    private TestJwt() {
    }

    public static String bearer(UUID userId) {
        return "Bearer " + token(userId, Instant.now().plusSeconds(60));
    }

    public static String token(UUID userId, Instant expiresAt) {
        try {
            JWTClaimsSet claims = new JWTClaimsSet.Builder()
                    .subject(userId.toString())
                    .issueTime(Date.from(expiresAt.minusSeconds(60)))
                    .expirationTime(Date.from(expiresAt))
                    .build();
            SignedJWT jwt = new SignedJWT(new JWSHeader(JWSAlgorithm.HS256), claims);
            jwt.sign(new MACSigner(SECRET));
            return jwt.serialize();
        } catch (JOSEException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(exception);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/ErrorCodeTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.EmailNotVerifiedException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.EmailVerificationTokenException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.InvalidCredentialsException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.InvalidPasswordPolicyException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.InvalidRefreshTokenException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.PasswordResetTokenException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.InsufficientBalanceException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.InvalidTransferException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.ReceiptAccessDeniedException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.ReceiptUnavailableException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransactionNotFoundException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.service.CnpjValidationUnavailableException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.service.DuplicateUserDataException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.service.InvalidDocumentException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.InvalidStatementQueryException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.WalletNotFoundException;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class ErrorCodeTest {

    @Test
    void shouldExposeTheCompleteCatalogWithHttpStatus() {
        Map<String, Integer> expected = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        expected.put("VALIDATION_ERROR", 400);
        expected.put("MALFORMED_REQUEST", 400);
        expected.put("INVALID_PARAMETER", 400);
        expected.put("INVALID_AMOUNT", 400);
        expected.put("INVALID_DOCUMENT", 400);
        expected.put("EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_INVALID", 400);
        expected.put("EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_EXPIRED", 400);
        expected.put("EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_ALREADY_USED", 400);
        expected.put("PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_INVALID", 400);
        expected.put("PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_EXPIRED", 400);
        expected.put("PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_ALREADY_USED", 400);
        expected.put("INVALID_PASSWORD_POLICY", 400);
        expected.put("INVALID_TRANSFER", 400);
        expected.put("INVALID_STATEMENT_QUERY", 400);
        expected.put("INVALID_LIMITS", 400);
        expected.put("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED", 400);
        expected.put("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID", 400);
        expected.put("UNAUTHORIZED", 401);
        expected.put("INVALID_CREDENTIALS", 401);
        expected.put("INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN", 401);
        expected.put("ACCESS_DENIED", 403);
        expected.put("EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED", 403);
        expected.put("RECEIPT_ACCESS_DENIED", 403);
        expected.put("ROUTE_NOT_FOUND", 404);
        expected.put("WALLET_NOT_FOUND", 404);
        expected.put("TRANSACTION_NOT_FOUND", 404);
        expected.put("METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED", 405);
        expected.put("DUPLICATE_USER_DATA", 409);
        expected.put("RECEIPT_UNAVAILABLE", 409);
        expected.put("BALANCE_CONFLICT", 409);
        expected.put("DATA_CONFLICT", 409);
        expected.put("UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE", 415);
        expected.put("INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE", 422);
        expected.put("LIMIT_EXCEEDED", 422);
        expected.put("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED", 422);
        expected.put("INTERNAL_ERROR", 500);
        expected.put("CNPJ_VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE", 503);

        Map<String, Integer> actual = Arrays.stream(ErrorCode.values())
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(Enum::name, ErrorCode::status, (a, b) -> a, LinkedHashMap::new));

        assertThat(actual).containsExactlyEntriesOf(expected);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldDescribeStatusesWithTheTextsTheApiAlreadyUses() {
        assertThat(ErrorCode.INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE.error()).isEqualTo("Unprocessable Entity");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR.error()).isEqualTo("Bad Request");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED.error()).isEqualTo("Unauthorized");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED.error()).isEqualTo("Forbidden");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.WALLET_NOT_FOUND.error()).isEqualTo("Not Found");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED.error()).isEqualTo("Method Not Allowed");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.BALANCE_CONFLICT.error()).isEqualTo("Conflict");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE.error()).isEqualTo("Unsupported Media Type");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR.error()).isEqualTo("Internal Server Error");
        assertThat(ErrorCode.CNPJ_VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE.error()).isEqualTo("Service Unavailable");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapEveryExistingBusinessExceptionToItsCatalogCode() {
        assertThat(new EmailNotVerifiedException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED);
        assertThat(new InvalidCredentialsException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_CREDENTIALS);
        assertThat(new InvalidRefreshTokenException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN);
        assertThat(new InvalidPasswordPolicyException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_PASSWORD_POLICY);
        assertThat(new EmailVerificationTokenException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_EXPIRED, "x").getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(ErrorCode.EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_EXPIRED);
        assertThat(new PasswordResetTokenException(ErrorCode.PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_ALREADY_USED, "x").getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(ErrorCode.PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_ALREADY_USED);
        assertThat(new InvalidDocumentException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_DOCUMENT);
        assertThat(new DuplicateUserDataException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.DUPLICATE_USER_DATA);
        assertThat(new CnpjValidationUnavailableException("x", null).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(ErrorCode.CNPJ_VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE);
        assertThat(new InvalidTransferException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_TRANSFER);
        assertThat(new InsufficientBalanceException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE);
        assertThat(new TransactionNotFoundException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.TRANSACTION_NOT_FOUND);
        assertThat(new ReceiptAccessDeniedException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.RECEIPT_ACCESS_DENIED);
        assertThat(new ReceiptUnavailableException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.RECEIPT_UNAVAILABLE);
        assertThat(new WalletNotFoundException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.WALLET_NOT_FOUND);
        assertThat(new InvalidStatementQueryException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_STATEMENT_QUERY);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldKeepFieldDetailsOfDocumentAndPasswordErrors() {
        assertThat(new InvalidDocumentException("CPF invalido").getFields())
                .containsExactlyEntriesOf(Map.of("document", "CPF invalido"));
        assertThat(new InvalidPasswordPolicyException("Senha fraca").getFields())
                .containsExactlyEntriesOf(Map.of("newPassword", "Senha fraca"));
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.containsString;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.not;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.content;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.config.SecurityConfig;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestJwt;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.InsufficientBalanceException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.service.InvalidDocumentException;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.webmvc.test.autoconfigure.WebMvcTest;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.context.TestPropertySource;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@WebMvcTest(GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.ProbeController.class)
@Import({SecurityConfig.class, GlobalExceptionHandlerTest.ProbeController.class})
@TestPropertySource(properties = "wallet-pay.auth.jwt-secret=" + TestJwt.SECRET)
class GlobalExceptionHandlerTest {

    @Autowired
    private MockMvc mockMvc;

    private String auth() {
        return TestJwt.bearer(UUID.randomUUID());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapBusinessExceptionUsingItsErrorCode() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/business").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value(422))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.error").value("Unprocessable Entity"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Saldo insuficiente"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields").isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldKeepBusinessExceptionFields() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/document").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INVALID_DOCUMENT"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.document").value("CPF invalido"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldHideUnexpectedErrorDetails() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/boom").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isInternalServerError())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INTERNAL_ERROR"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Erro interno, informe o requestId ao suporte"))
                .andExpect(content().string(not(containsString("detalhe interno"))))
                .andExpect(content().string(not(containsString("IllegalStateException"))));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapDataIntegrityViolationWithoutConstraintName() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/integrity").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("DATA_CONFLICT"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Conflito de dados"))
                .andExpect(content().string(not(containsString("uk_users_email"))));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapMalformedJson() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/body")
                        .header("Authorization", auth())
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("{"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("MALFORMED_REQUEST"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Corpo da requisicao invalido"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapUnsupportedMediaType() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/probe/body")
                        .header("Authorization", auth())
                        .contentType(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN)
                        .content("texto"))
                .andExpect(status().isUnsupportedMediaType())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapParameterTypeMismatch() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/param").param("count", "abc").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INVALID_PARAMETER"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.count").value("Valor invalido"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapMissingParameter() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/param").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INVALID_PARAMETER"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.count").value("Parametro obrigatorio"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapMissingIdempotencyKeyHeader() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/idempotent").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Idempotency-Key e obrigatorio"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapOtherMissingHeader() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/header").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INVALID_PARAMETER"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.X-Probe").value("Header obrigatorio"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapMethodNotAllowed() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(delete("/probe/business").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isMethodNotAllowed())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapUnknownRoute() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/probe/nao-existe").header("Authorization", auth()))
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("ROUTE_NOT_FOUND"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Rota nao encontrada"));
    }

    @RestController
    @RequestMapping("/probe")
    static class ProbeController {

        @GetMapping("/business")
        void business() {
            throw new InsufficientBalanceException("Saldo insuficiente");
        }

        @GetMapping("/document")
        void document() {
            throw new InvalidDocumentException("CPF invalido");
        }

        @GetMapping("/boom")
        void boom() {
            throw new IllegalStateException("select * from users -- detalhe interno");
        }

        @GetMapping("/integrity")
        void integrity() {
            throw new DataIntegrityViolationException("violates unique constraint uk_users_email");
        }

        @PostMapping(value = "/body", consumes = MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)
        void body(@RequestBody ProbeBody body) {
        }

        @GetMapping("/param")
        void param(@RequestParam int count) {
        }

        @GetMapping("/header")
        void header(@RequestHeader("X-Probe") String value) {
        }

        @GetMapping("/idempotent")
        void idempotent(@RequestHeader("Idempotency-Key") String value) {
        }

        record ProbeBody(String value) {
        }
    }
}
```

Classes aninhadas em classes de teste ficam fora do component scan do Spring Boot. Por isso o `ProbeController` precisa do `@Import`.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/ErrorCode.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

public enum ErrorCode {
    VALIDATION_ERROR(400),
    MALFORMED_REQUEST(400),
    INVALID_PARAMETER(400),
    INVALID_AMOUNT(400),
    INVALID_DOCUMENT(400),
    EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_INVALID(400),
    EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_EXPIRED(400),
    EMAIL_VERIFICATION_TOKEN_ALREADY_USED(400),
    PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_INVALID(400),
    PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_EXPIRED(400),
    PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_ALREADY_USED(400),
    INVALID_PASSWORD_POLICY(400),
    INVALID_TRANSFER(400),
    INVALID_STATEMENT_QUERY(400),
    INVALID_LIMITS(400),
    IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED(400),
    IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID(400),
    UNAUTHORIZED(401),
    INVALID_CREDENTIALS(401),
    INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN(401),
    ACCESS_DENIED(403),
    EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED(403),
    RECEIPT_ACCESS_DENIED(403),
    ROUTE_NOT_FOUND(404),
    WALLET_NOT_FOUND(404),
    TRANSACTION_NOT_FOUND(404),
    METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED(405),
    DUPLICATE_USER_DATA(409),
    RECEIPT_UNAVAILABLE(409),
    BALANCE_CONFLICT(409),
    DATA_CONFLICT(409),
    UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE(415),
    INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE(422),
    LIMIT_EXCEEDED(422),
    IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED(422),
    INTERNAL_ERROR(500),
    CNPJ_VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE(503);

    private final int status;

    ErrorCode(int status) {
        this.status = status;
    }

    public int status() {
        return status;
    }

    public String error() {
        return switch (status) {
            case 400 -> "Bad Request";
            case 401 -> "Unauthorized";
            case 403 -> "Forbidden";
            case 404 -> "Not Found";
            case 405 -> "Method Not Allowed";
            case 409 -> "Conflict";
            case 415 -> "Unsupported Media Type";
            case 422 -> "Unprocessable Entity";
            case 500 -> "Internal Server Error";
            case 503 -> "Service Unavailable";
            default -> throw new IllegalStateException("Status sem descricao: " + status);
        };
    }
}
```

O texto de 422 fica fixo em `"Unprocessable Entity"` porque o Spring 7 mudou a frase padrao para "Unprocessable Content" e os testes atuais esperam o texto antigo.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/BusinessException.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import java.util.Map;

public abstract class BusinessException extends RuntimeException {

    private final ErrorCode errorCode;

    protected BusinessException(ErrorCode errorCode, String message) {
        super(message);
        this.errorCode = errorCode;
    }

    protected BusinessException(ErrorCode errorCode, String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
        this.errorCode = errorCode;
    }

    public ErrorCode getErrorCode() {
        return errorCode;
    }

    public Map<String, String> getFields() {
        return Map.of();
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import java.util.Map;

public record ApiErrorResponse(
        int status,
        String error,
        String code,
        String message,
        Map<String, String> fields
) {
    public static ApiErrorResponse of(ErrorCode code, String message, Map<String, String> fields) {
        return new ApiErrorResponse(
                code.status(),
                code.error(),
                code.name(),
                message,
                fields == null ? Map.of() : fields
        );
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/GlobalExceptionHandler.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.http.converter.HttpMessageNotReadableException;
import org.springframework.web.HttpMediaTypeNotSupportedException;
import org.springframework.web.HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MethodArgumentNotValidException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MissingRequestHeaderException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MissingServletRequestParameterException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;
import org.springframework.web.method.annotation.MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.NoHandlerFoundException;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.resource.NoResourceFoundException;

@RestControllerAdvice
public class GlobalExceptionHandler {

    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GlobalExceptionHandler.class);
    private static final String IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_HEADER = "Idempotency-Key";

    @ExceptionHandler(BusinessException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleBusiness(BusinessException exception) {
        LOGGER.warn("Erro de negocio {}: {}", exception.getErrorCode(), exception.getMessage());
        return error(exception.getErrorCode(), exception.getMessage(), exception.getFields());
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleValidation(MethodArgumentNotValidException exception) {
        Map<String, String> fields = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        exception.getBindingResult().getFieldErrors().forEach(error ->
                fields.putIfAbsent(error.getField(), error.getDefaultMessage())
        );
        return error(ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR, "Dados invalidos", fields);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleMalformedRequest(HttpMessageNotReadableException exception) {
        return error(ErrorCode.MALFORMED_REQUEST, "Corpo da requisicao invalido", Map.of());
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleTypeMismatch(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException exception) {
        return error(ErrorCode.INVALID_PARAMETER, "Parametro invalido", Map.of(exception.getName(), "Valor invalido"));
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(MissingServletRequestParameterException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleMissingParameter(MissingServletRequestParameterException exception) {
        return error(
                ErrorCode.INVALID_PARAMETER,
                "Parametro obrigatorio ausente",
                Map.of(exception.getParameterName(), "Parametro obrigatorio")
        );
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(MissingRequestHeaderException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleMissingHeader(MissingRequestHeaderException exception) {
        if (IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_HEADER.equalsIgnoreCase(exception.getHeaderName())) {
            return error(ErrorCode.IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED, "Idempotency-Key e obrigatorio", Map.of());
        }
        return error(
                ErrorCode.INVALID_PARAMETER,
                "Header obrigatorio ausente",
                Map.of(exception.getHeaderName(), "Header obrigatorio")
        );
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleMethodNotAllowed(HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException exception) {
        return error(ErrorCode.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED, "Metodo HTTP nao suportado", Map.of());
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMediaTypeNotSupportedException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleUnsupportedMediaType(HttpMediaTypeNotSupportedException exception) {
        return error(ErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE, "Content-Type nao suportado", Map.of());
    }

    @ExceptionHandler({NoResourceFoundException.class, NoHandlerFoundException.class})
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleRouteNotFound(Exception exception) {
        return error(ErrorCode.ROUTE_NOT_FOUND, "Rota nao encontrada", Map.of());
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(DataIntegrityViolationException.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleDataIntegrity(DataIntegrityViolationException exception) {
        LOGGER.warn("Violacao de integridade nao tratada", exception);
        return error(ErrorCode.DATA_CONFLICT, "Conflito de dados", Map.of());
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleUnexpected(Exception exception) {
        LOGGER.error("Erro inesperado", exception);
        return error(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "Erro interno, informe o requestId ao suporte", Map.of());
    }

    static ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> error(ErrorCode code, String message, Map<String, String> fields) {
        return ResponseEntity.status(code.status()).body(ApiErrorResponse.of(code, message, fields));
    }
}
```

Cada classe abaixo passa a estender `BusinessException`, mantendo pacote, nome e construtor publico atuais. Modelo para as que so recebem mensagem:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.web.RequestIdFilter;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.slf4j.MDC;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockHttpServletRequest;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockHttpServletResponse;
import org.springframework.security.access.AccessDeniedException;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

class ApiAccessDeniedHandlerTest {

    @AfterEach
    void clearMdc() {
        MDC.clear();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldWriteForbiddenAsApiError() throws Exception {
        MDC.put(RequestIdFilter.MDC_KEY, "req-403");
        MockHttpServletResponse response = new MockHttpServletResponse();

        new ApiAccessDeniedHandler(JsonMapper.builder().build())
                .handle(new MockHttpServletRequest(), response, new AccessDeniedException("negado"));

        assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(response.getContentType()).startsWith("application/json");
        assertThat(response.getContentAsString())
                .contains("\"code\":\"ACCESS_DENIED\"")
                .contains("\"message\":\"Acesso negado\"")
                .contains("\"requestId\":\"req-403\"");
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.config;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.infra.CnpjWsValidatorGateway;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.service.CnpjValidationUnavailableException;
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class HttpClientConfigTest {

    private HttpServer slowCnpjService;

    @BeforeEach
    void startSlowCnpjService() throws IOException {
        slowCnpjService = HttpServer.create(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", 0), 0);
        slowCnpjService.createContext("/cnpj/", exchange -> {
            try {
                Thread.sleep(6_000);
            } catch (InterruptedException exception) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
            exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, -1);
            exchange.close();
        });
        slowCnpjService.setExecutor(Executors.newCachedThreadPool());
        slowCnpjService.start();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void stopSlowCnpjService() {
        slowCnpjService.stop(0);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldGiveUpOnSlowCnpjServiceAfterTheReadTimeout() {
        CnpjWsValidatorGateway gateway = new CnpjWsValidatorGateway(
                new HttpClientConfig().restClientBuilder(),
                "http://127.0.0.1:" + slowCnpjService.getAddress().getPort()
        );
        long start = System.nanoTime();

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> gateway.exists("11222333000181"))
                .isInstanceOf(CnpjValidationUnavailableException.class)
                .hasMessage("Tempo esgotado ao validar o CNPJ");

        Duration elapsed = Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start);
        assertThat(elapsed).isBetween(Duration.ofMillis(2_500), Duration.ofMillis(5_000));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Criar o `RequestIdFilter`**

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/web/RequestIdFilter.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.web;

import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
import org.slf4j.MDC;
import org.springframework.core.Ordered;
import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.filter.OncePerRequestFilter;

@Component
@Order(Ordered.HIGHEST_PRECEDENCE)
public class RequestIdFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Request-Id";
    public static final String MDC_KEY = "requestId";

    private static final Pattern VALID_REQUEST_ID = Pattern.compile("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$");

    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response,
            FilterChain filterChain
    ) throws ServletException, IOException {
        String requestId = resolve(request.getHeader(HEADER));
        MDC.put(MDC_KEY, requestId);
        response.setHeader(HEADER, requestId);
        try {
            filterChain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            MDC.remove(MDC_KEY);
        }
    }

    static String resolve(String candidate) {
        if (candidate != null && VALID_REQUEST_ID.matcher(candidate).matches()) {
            return candidate;
        }
        return UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }
}
```

`@Order(Ordered.HIGHEST_PRECEDENCE)` coloca o filtro antes do filtro do Spring Security (ordem -100), entao o `requestId` ja existe quando o 401 e escrito.

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.web.RequestIdFilter;
import java.util.Map;
import org.slf4j.MDC;

public record ApiErrorResponse(
        int status,
        String error,
        String code,
        String message,
        Map<String, String> fields,
        String requestId
) {
    public static ApiErrorResponse of(ErrorCode code, String message, Map<String, String> fields) {
        return new ApiErrorResponse(
                code.status(),
                code.error(),
                code.name(),
                message,
                fields == null ? Map.of() : fields,
                MDC.get(RequestIdFilter.MDC_KEY)
        );
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Criar o escritor de erro e os handlers de seguranca**

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/ApiErrorWriter.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Map;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

final class ApiErrorWriter {

    private ApiErrorWriter() {
    }

    static void write(
            HttpServletResponse response,
            JsonMapper jsonMapper,
            ErrorCode code,
            String message
    ) throws IOException {
        response.setStatus(code.status());
        response.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE);
        response.setCharacterEncoding(StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name());
        jsonMapper.writeValue(response.getOutputStream(), ApiErrorResponse.of(code, message, Map.of()));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/ApiAuthenticationEntryPoint.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;
import org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.server.resource.web.BearerTokenAuthenticationEntryPoint;
import org.springframework.security.web.AuthenticationEntryPoint;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

public class ApiAuthenticationEntryPoint implements AuthenticationEntryPoint {

    private final AuthenticationEntryPoint bearerEntryPoint = new BearerTokenAuthenticationEntryPoint();
    private final JsonMapper jsonMapper;

    public ApiAuthenticationEntryPoint(JsonMapper jsonMapper) {
        this.jsonMapper = jsonMapper;
    }

    @Override
    public void commence(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response,
            AuthenticationException authException
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {
        bearerEntryPoint.commence(request, response, authException);
        ApiErrorWriter.write(response, jsonMapper, ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, "Token ausente, invalido ou expirado");
    }
}
```

O `BearerTokenAuthenticationEntryPoint` escreve o header `WWW-Authenticate`; o corpo vem do `ApiErrorWriter`.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/error/ApiAccessDeniedHandler.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;
import org.springframework.security.access.AccessDeniedException;
import org.springframework.security.web.access.AccessDeniedHandler;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

public class ApiAccessDeniedHandler implements AccessDeniedHandler {

    private final JsonMapper jsonMapper;

    public ApiAccessDeniedHandler(JsonMapper jsonMapper) {
        this.jsonMapper = jsonMapper;
    }

    @Override
    public void handle(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response,
            AccessDeniedException accessDeniedException
    ) throws IOException {
        ApiErrorWriter.write(response, jsonMapper, ErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED, "Acesso negado");
    }
}
```

```java
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ApiAuthenticationEntryPoint;
```

E substituir o metodo `securityFilterChain` por:

```java
    @Bean
    SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http, JsonMapper jsonMapper) throws Exception {
        ApiAuthenticationEntryPoint authenticationEntryPoint = new ApiAuthenticationEntryPoint(jsonMapper);
        ApiAccessDeniedHandler accessDeniedHandler = new ApiAccessDeniedHandler(jsonMapper);

        return http
                .csrf(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable)
                .sessionManagement(session -> session.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS))
                .authorizeHttpRequests(authorize -> authorize
                        .requestMatchers("/users").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/auth/login").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/auth/refresh").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/auth/email-verification/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/auth/password-recovery/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/actuator/health").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/swagger-ui.html").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/swagger-ui/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/v3/api-docs/**").permitAll()
                        .anyRequest().authenticated()
                )
                .exceptionHandling(exceptions -> exceptions
                        .authenticationEntryPoint(authenticationEntryPoint)
                        .accessDeniedHandler(accessDeniedHandler)
                )
                .oauth2ResourceServer(oauth2 -> oauth2
                        .jwt(Customizer.withDefaults())
                        .authenticationEntryPoint(authenticationEntryPoint)
                        .accessDeniedHandler(accessDeniedHandler)
                )
                .build();
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Timeouts do cliente de CNPJ**

Substituir `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/config/HttpClientConfig.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.config;

import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.time.Duration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.http.client.JdkClientHttpRequestFactory;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;

@Configuration
public class HttpClientConfig {

    static final Duration CONNECT_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(2);
    static final Duration READ_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(3);

    @Bean
    RestClient.Builder restClientBuilder() {
        HttpClient httpClient = HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(CONNECT_TIMEOUT)
                .build();
        JdkClientHttpRequestFactory requestFactory = new JdkClientHttpRequestFactory(httpClient);
        requestFactory.setReadTimeout(READ_TIMEOUT);
        return RestClient.builder().requestFactory(requestFactory);
    }
}
```

Em `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/user/infra/CnpjWsValidatorGateway.java`, adicionar `import java.net.http.HttpTimeoutException;`, trocar o bloco `catch (RestClientException exception)` por:

```java
        } catch (RestClientException exception) {
            if (isTimeout(exception)) {
                throw new CnpjValidationUnavailableException("Tempo esgotado ao validar o CNPJ", exception);
            }
            throw new CnpjValidationUnavailableException("Nao foi possivel validar o CNPJ", exception);
        }
```

e adicionar o metodo no fim da classe:

```java
    private static boolean isTimeout(Throwable exception) {
        for (Throwable cause = exception; cause != null; cause = cause.getCause()) {
            if (cause instanceof SocketTimeoutException || cause instanceof HttpTimeoutException) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
```

O cliente JDK lanca `HttpTimeoutException`, nao `SocketTimeoutException`. O 503 sai do handler de `BusinessException` (Task 2).

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.CsvSource;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;

class MoneyTest {

    @ParameterizedTest
    @CsvSource({
            "10, 10.00",
            "10.5, 10.50",
            "' 25.50 ', 25.50",
            "0.01, 0.01",
            "99999999999999999.99, 99999999999999999.99"
    })
    void shouldParseValidAmountsWithScaleTwo(String raw, String expected) {
        BigDecimal amount = Money.parse(raw);

        assertThat(amount).isEqualByComparingTo(expected);
        assertThat(amount.scale()).isEqualTo(2);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectMissingAmount() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> Money.parse(null))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidAmountException.class)
                .hasMessage("Valor e obrigatorio");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> Money.parse("   "))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidAmountException.class)
                .hasMessage("Valor e obrigatorio");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"abc", "10,50", "1e999999999999"})
    void shouldRejectNonNumericAmount(String raw) {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> Money.parse(raw))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidAmountException.class)
                .hasMessage("Valor invalido");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"0", "0.00", "-1", "-0.01"})
    void shouldRejectAmountNotGreaterThanZero(String raw) {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> Money.parse(raw))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidAmountException.class)
                .hasMessage("Valor deve ser maior que zero");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectMoreThanTwoDecimalPlaces() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> Money.parse("10.123"))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidAmountException.class)
                .hasMessage("Valor deve ter no maximo duas casas decimais");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"100000000000000000", "100000000000000000000", "1E+30"})
    void shouldRejectAmountsThatDoNotFitTheDatabaseColumn(String raw) {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> Money.parse(raw))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidAmountException.class)
                .hasMessage("Valor invalido");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldUseInvalidAmountCode() {
        assertThat(new InvalidAmountException("x").getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_AMOUNT);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldFormatWithTwoDecimalPlaces() {
        assertThat(Money.format(new BigDecimal("10"))).isEqualTo("10.00");
        assertThat(Money.format(new BigDecimal("0.5"))).isEqualTo("0.50");
        assertThat(Money.format(new BigDecimal("1E+3"))).isEqualTo("1000.00");
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.BusinessException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;

public class InvalidAmountException extends BusinessException {

    public InvalidAmountException(String message) {
        super(ErrorCode.INVALID_AMOUNT, message);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/money/Money.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;

public final class Money {

    private static final int SCALE = 2;
    private static final int MAX_INTEGER_DIGITS = 17;

    private Money() {
    }

    public static BigDecimal parse(String raw) {
        if (raw == null || raw.isBlank()) {
            throw new InvalidAmountException("Valor e obrigatorio");
        }

        BigDecimal amount;
        try {
            amount = new BigDecimal(raw.trim());
        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
            throw new InvalidAmountException("Valor invalido");
        }

        if (amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
            throw new InvalidAmountException("Valor deve ser maior que zero");
        }
        if (amount.scale() > SCALE) {
            throw new InvalidAmountException("Valor deve ter no maximo duas casas decimais");
        }
        if (amount.precision() - amount.scale() > MAX_INTEGER_DIGITS) {
            throw new InvalidAmountException("Valor invalido");
        }
        return amount.setScale(SCALE, RoundingMode.UNNECESSARY);
    }

    public static String format(BigDecimal amount) {
        return amount.setScale(SCALE, RoundingMode.UNNECESSARY).toPlainString();
    }
}
```

`MAX_INTEGER_DIGITS = 17` corresponde a `NUMERIC(19, 2)`.

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.config.ResilienceConfig;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionStatus;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionType;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.BalanceConflictException;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.dao.OptimisticLockingFailureException;

class TransferServiceTest {

    private static final TransferCommand COMMAND = new TransferCommand(
            UUID.randomUUID(),
            UUID.randomUUID(),
            "10.00",
            null
    );

    @Test
    void shouldRetryOptimisticLockingFailuresUntilSuccess() {
        FakeCreateTransferUseCase useCase = new FakeCreateTransferUseCase(2);
        TransferService service = new TransferService(new ResilienceConfig().balanceRetryTemplate(), useCase);

        TransferResult result = service.transfer(COMMAND);

        assertThat(result).isSameAs(useCase.result);
        assertThat(useCase.calls).isEqualTo(3);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldGiveUpAfterFourCallsWithBalanceConflict() {
        FakeCreateTransferUseCase useCase = new FakeCreateTransferUseCase(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        TransferService service = new TransferService(new ResilienceConfig().balanceRetryTemplate(), useCase);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.transfer(COMMAND))
                .isInstanceOf(BalanceConflictException.class)
                .hasMessage("Operacao concorrente na carteira, tente novamente");
        assertThat(useCase.calls).isEqualTo(4);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotRetryBusinessErrors() {
        FakeCreateTransferUseCase useCase = new FakeCreateTransferUseCase(0);
        useCase.businessError = new InsufficientBalanceException("Saldo insuficiente");
        TransferService service = new TransferService(new ResilienceConfig().balanceRetryTemplate(), useCase);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.transfer(COMMAND))
                .isInstanceOf(InsufficientBalanceException.class);
        assertThat(useCase.calls).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    static class FakeCreateTransferUseCase extends CreateTransferUseCase {

        private final int failuresBeforeSuccess;
        private final TransferResult result = new TransferResult(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                TransactionType.TRANSFER,
                TransactionStatus.COMPLETED,
                "10.00",
                "BRL",
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                null,
                Instant.now(),
                Instant.now()
        );
        private RuntimeException businessError;
        private int calls;

        FakeCreateTransferUseCase(int failuresBeforeSuccess) {
            super(null, null);
            this.failuresBeforeSuccess = failuresBeforeSuccess;
        }

        @Override
        public TransferResult transfer(TransferCommand command) {
            calls++;
            if (businessError != null) {
                throw businessError;
            }
            if (calls <= failuresBeforeSuccess) {
                throw new OptimisticLockingFailureException("versao da carteira mudou");
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/config/ResilienceConfig.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.config;

import java.time.Duration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.core.retry.RetryPolicy;
import org.springframework.core.retry.RetryTemplate;
import org.springframework.dao.OptimisticLockingFailureException;

@Configuration
public class ResilienceConfig {

    @Bean
    public RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate() {
        RetryPolicy policy = RetryPolicy.builder()
                .includes(OptimisticLockingFailureException.class)
                .maxRetries(3)
                .delay(Duration.ofMillis(50))
                .jitter(Duration.ofMillis(25))
                .build();
        return new RetryTemplate(policy);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/BalanceConflictException.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.BusinessException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;

public class BalanceConflictException extends BusinessException {

    public BalanceConflictException(String message) {
        super(ErrorCode.BALANCE_CONFLICT, message);
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.BalanceConflictException;
import org.springframework.core.retry.RetryTemplate;
import org.springframework.dao.OptimisticLockingFailureException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
public class TransferService {

    private final RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate;
    private final CreateTransferUseCase createTransferUseCase;

    public TransferService(RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate, CreateTransferUseCase createTransferUseCase) {
        this.balanceRetryTemplate = balanceRetryTemplate;
        this.createTransferUseCase = createTransferUseCase;
    }

    public TransferResult transfer(TransferCommand command) {
        try {
            return balanceRetryTemplate.invoke(() -> createTransferUseCase.transfer(command));
        } catch (OptimisticLockingFailureException exception) {
            throw new BalanceConflictException("Operacao concorrente na carteira, tente novamente");
        }
    }
}
```

`RetryTemplate.invoke` relanca a excecao original: a ultima `OptimisticLockingFailureException` quando as tentativas acabam, ou, na hora, qualquer excecao fora de `includes`. `TransferService` nao tem `@Transactional`; cada tentativa abre uma transacao nova no proxy de `CreateTransferUseCase`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Escrever o teste de concorrencia**

Em `src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/support/AbstractIntegrationTest.java`, adicionar os imports `java.util.ArrayList`, `java.util.List`, `java.util.concurrent.Callable`, `java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch`, `java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService`, `java.util.concurrent.Executors`, `java.util.concurrent.Future`, `java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit` e o metodo:

```java
    protected <T> List<T> runConcurrently(int threads, Callable<T> task) throws Exception {
        ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(threads);
        CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
        try {
            List<Future<T>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < threads; i++) {
                futures.add(executor.submit(() -> {
                    start.await();
                    return task.call();
                }));
            }
            start.countDown();

            List<T> results = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Future<T> future : futures) {
                results.add(future.get(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS));
            }
            return results;
        } finally {
            executor.shutdownNow();
        }
    }
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/transaction/TransferConcurrencyIntegrationTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestUser;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.InsufficientBalanceException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.BalanceConflictException;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;

class TransferConcurrencyIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private TransferService transferService;

    @Test
    void shouldKeepBalancesCorrectUnderConcurrentTransfers() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");
        BigDecimal totalBefore = totalBalance();

        List<String> outcomes = runConcurrently(20, () -> transferAsClient(sender, receiver, "10.00"));

        assertThat(outcomes).filteredOn("SUCCESS"::equals).hasSize(10);
        assertThat(outcomes).filteredOn("INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE"::equals).hasSize(10);
        assertThat(balanceOf(sender)).isEqualByComparingTo("0.00");
        assertThat(balanceOf(receiver)).isEqualByComparingTo("100.00");
        assertThat(totalBalance()).isEqualByComparingTo(totalBefore);
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from wallets where balance < 0", Long.class)).isZero();
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from transactions", Long.class)).isEqualTo(10L);
    }

    private String transferAsClient(TestUser sender, TestUser receiver, String amount) {
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++) {
            try {
                transferService.transfer(new TransferCommand(sender.userId(), receiver.userId(), amount, null));
                return "SUCCESS";
            } catch (InsufficientBalanceException exception) {
                return "INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE";
            } catch (BalanceConflictException exception) {
                // o cliente repete depois de 409 BALANCE_CONFLICT
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("Transferencia nao terminou depois de 50 tentativas do cliente");
    }
}
```

O loop do cliente e necessario: com 20 threads na mesma carteira, uma chamada pode esgotar as 4 tentativas internas e receber 409, que e o comportamento correto. O teste confere as invariantes da spec: 10 sucessos, origem zerada, destino com `100.00`, nenhum saldo negativo e soma preservada.

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/CreateDepositUseCaseTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.InvalidAmountException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionStatus;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionType;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.repository.TransactionRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.domain.UserEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Proxy;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class CreateDepositUseCaseTest {

    private static final Instant NOW = Instant.parse("2026-10-15T13:00:00Z");
    private static final Clock CLOCK = Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC);

    @Test
    void shouldCreditOwnWalletAndRegisterDeposit() {
        UUID userId = UUID.randomUUID();
        WalletEntity wallet = wallet(userId, "100.00");
        FakeTransactionRepository transactionRepository = new FakeTransactionRepository();
        CreateDepositUseCase useCase = useCase(new FakeWalletRepository(wallet).proxy(), transactionRepository.proxy());

        DepositResult result = useCase.deposit(new DepositCommand(userId, "150", "  Aporte inicial "));

        assertThat(wallet.getBalance()).isEqualByComparingTo("250.00");
        TransactionEntity saved = transactionRepository.savedTransaction;
        assertThat(saved.getType()).isEqualTo(TransactionType.DEPOSIT);
        assertThat(saved.getSenderWallet()).isNull();
        assertThat(saved.getReceiverWallet()).isSameAs(wallet);
        assertThat(saved.getCreatedAt()).isEqualTo(NOW);
        assertThat(result.transactionId()).isEqualTo(transactionRepository.transactionId);
        assertThat(result.type()).isEqualTo(TransactionType.DEPOSIT);
        assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(TransactionStatus.COMPLETED);
        assertThat(result.amount()).isEqualTo("150.00");
        assertThat(result.currency()).isEqualTo("BRL");
        assertThat(result.description()).isEqualTo("Aporte inicial");
        assertThat(result.wallet().id()).isEqualTo(wallet.getId());
        assertThat(result.wallet().balance()).isEqualTo("250.00");
        assertThat(result.createdAt()).isEqualTo(NOW);
        assertThat(result.completedAt()).isEqualTo(NOW);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectInvalidAmountWithoutTouchingWallet() {
        UUID userId = UUID.randomUUID();
        WalletEntity wallet = wallet(userId, "100.00");
        FakeTransactionRepository transactionRepository = new FakeTransactionRepository();
        CreateDepositUseCase useCase = useCase(new FakeWalletRepository(wallet).proxy(), transactionRepository.proxy());

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> useCase.deposit(new DepositCommand(userId, "0", null)))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidAmountException.class)
                .hasMessage("Valor deve ser maior que zero");
        assertThat(wallet.getBalance()).isEqualByComparingTo("100.00");
        assertThat(transactionRepository.savedTransaction).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldFailWhenWalletDoesNotExist() {
        CreateDepositUseCase useCase = useCase(
                new FakeWalletRepository(null).proxy(),
                new FakeTransactionRepository().proxy()
        );

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> useCase.deposit(new DepositCommand(UUID.randomUUID(), "10.00", null)))
                .isInstanceOf(WalletNotFoundException.class)
                .hasMessage("Carteira nao encontrada para o usuario autenticado");
    }

    private static CreateDepositUseCase useCase(WalletRepository walletRepository, TransactionRepository transactionRepository) {
        return new CreateDepositUseCase(walletRepository, transactionRepository, CLOCK);
    }

    private static WalletEntity wallet(UUID userId, String balance) {
        UserEntity user = new UserEntity("Joao Silva", userId + "@email.com", "hash", "52998224725");
        setField(user, "id", userId);
        WalletEntity wallet = new WalletEntity(user);
        setField(wallet, "id", UUID.randomUUID());
        setField(wallet, "balance", new BigDecimal(balance));
        return wallet;
    }

    private static void setField(Object target, String fieldName, Object value) {
        try {
            Field field = target.getClass().getDeclaredField(fieldName);
            field.setAccessible(true);
            field.set(target, value);
        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException exception) {
            throw new AssertionError(exception);
        }
    }

    private static final class FakeWalletRepository {

        private final WalletEntity wallet;

        private FakeWalletRepository(WalletEntity wallet) {
            this.wallet = wallet;
        }

        private WalletRepository proxy() {
            return (WalletRepository) Proxy.newProxyInstance(
                    WalletRepository.class.getClassLoader(),
                    new Class<?>[] {WalletRepository.class},
                    (proxy, method, args) -> switch (method.getName()) {
                        case "findByUserId" -> Optional.ofNullable(wallet);
                        default -> throw new UnsupportedOperationException(method.getName());
                    }
            );
        }
    }

    private static final class FakeTransactionRepository {

        private final UUID transactionId = UUID.randomUUID();
        private TransactionEntity savedTransaction;

        private TransactionRepository proxy() {
            return (TransactionRepository) Proxy.newProxyInstance(
                    TransactionRepository.class.getClassLoader(),
                    new Class<?>[] {TransactionRepository.class},
                    (proxy, method, args) -> switch (method.getName()) {
                        case "saveAndFlush" -> {
                            savedTransaction = (TransactionEntity) args[0];
                            setField(savedTransaction, "id", transactionId);
                            yield savedTransaction;
                        }
                        default -> throw new UnsupportedOperationException(method.getName());
                    }
            );
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/DepositServiceTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.config.ResilienceConfig;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.dao.OptimisticLockingFailureException;

class DepositServiceTest {

    private static final DepositCommand COMMAND = new DepositCommand(UUID.randomUUID(), "10.00", null);

    @Test
    void shouldRetryAndThenReturnTheDeposit() {
        FakeCreateDepositUseCase useCase = new FakeCreateDepositUseCase(1);
        DepositService service = new DepositService(new ResilienceConfig().balanceRetryTemplate(), useCase);

        assertThat(service.deposit(COMMAND)).isSameAs(useCase.result);
        assertThat(useCase.calls).isEqualTo(2);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldTranslateExhaustedRetriesIntoBalanceConflict() {
        FakeCreateDepositUseCase useCase = new FakeCreateDepositUseCase(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        DepositService service = new DepositService(new ResilienceConfig().balanceRetryTemplate(), useCase);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.deposit(COMMAND))
                .isInstanceOf(BalanceConflictException.class)
                .hasMessage("Operacao concorrente na carteira, tente novamente");
        assertThat(useCase.calls).isEqualTo(4);
    }

    static class FakeCreateDepositUseCase extends CreateDepositUseCase {

        private final int failuresBeforeSuccess;
        private final DepositResult result = new DepositResult(
                UUID.randomUUID(), null, null, "10.00", "BRL", null, null, null, null
        );
        private int calls;

        FakeCreateDepositUseCase(int failuresBeforeSuccess) {
            super(null, null, null);
            this.failuresBeforeSuccess = failuresBeforeSuccess;
        }

        @Override
        public DepositResult deposit(DepositCommand command) {
            calls++;
            if (calls <= failuresBeforeSuccess) {
                throw new OptimisticLockingFailureException("versao da carteira mudou");
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
}
```
