Substituir `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/transaction/domain/TransactionEntity.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.EnumType;
import jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;
import jakarta.persistence.FetchType;
import jakarta.persistence.GeneratedValue;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.JoinColumn;
import jakarta.persistence.ManyToOne;
import jakarta.persistence.PrePersist;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.hibernate.annotations.UuidGenerator;

@Entity
@Table(name = "transactions")
public class TransactionEntity {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue
    @UuidGenerator
    private UUID id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "sender_wallet_id")
    private WalletEntity senderWallet;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "receiver_wallet_id", nullable = false)
    private WalletEntity receiverWallet;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private TransactionType type;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private TransactionStatus status;

    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 19, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal amount;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 3)
    private String currency;

    @Column(length = 255)
    private String description;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private Instant createdAt;

    @Column(name = "completed_at")
    private Instant completedAt;

    @Column(name = "failed_at")
    private Instant failedAt;

    @Column(name = "failure_reason", length = 255)
    private String failureReason;

    protected TransactionEntity() {
    }

    private TransactionEntity(
            TransactionType type,
            WalletEntity senderWallet,
            WalletEntity receiverWallet,
            BigDecimal amount,
            String description,
            Instant occurredAt
    ) {
        this.type = type;
        this.senderWallet = senderWallet;
        this.receiverWallet = receiverWallet;
        this.status = TransactionStatus.COMPLETED;
        this.amount = amount;
        this.currency = WalletEntity.DEFAULT_CURRENCY;
        this.description = description;
        this.createdAt = occurredAt;
        this.completedAt = occurredAt;
    }

    public static TransactionEntity completedTransfer(
            WalletEntity senderWallet,
            WalletEntity receiverWallet,
            BigDecimal amount,
            String description,
            Instant occurredAt
    ) {
        return new TransactionEntity(TransactionType.TRANSFER, senderWallet, receiverWallet, amount, description, occurredAt);
    }

    public static TransactionEntity completedDeposit(
            WalletEntity receiverWallet,
            BigDecimal amount,
            String description,
            Instant occurredAt
    ) {
        return new TransactionEntity(TransactionType.DEPOSIT, null, receiverWallet, amount, description, occurredAt);
    }

    @PrePersist
    void prePersist() {
        if (this.createdAt == null) {
            this.createdAt = Instant.now();
        }
        if (this.completedAt == null && this.status == TransactionStatus.COMPLETED) {
            this.completedAt = this.createdAt;
        }
    }

    public UUID getId() {
        return id;
    }

    public WalletEntity getSenderWallet() {
        return senderWallet;
    }

    public WalletEntity getReceiverWallet() {
        return receiverWallet;
    }

    public TransactionType getType() {
        return type;
    }

    public TransactionStatus getStatus() {
        return status;
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount() {
        return amount;
    }

    public String getCurrency() {
        return currency;
    }

    public String getDescription() {
        return description;
    }

    public Instant getCreatedAt() {
        return createdAt;
    }

    public Instant getCompletedAt() {
        return completedAt;
    }

    public Instant getFailedAt() {
        return failedAt;
    }

    public String getFailureReason() {
        return failureReason;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Transferencia com `Clock` e `TransactionDescription`**

Substituir `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/transaction/service/CreateTransferUseCase.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.Money;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionDescription;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.repository.TransactionRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.WalletNotFoundException;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Clock;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class CreateTransferUseCase {

    private final WalletRepository walletRepository;
    private final TransactionRepository transactionRepository;
    private final Clock clock;

    public CreateTransferUseCase(
            WalletRepository walletRepository,
            TransactionRepository transactionRepository,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.walletRepository = walletRepository;
        this.transactionRepository = transactionRepository;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Transactional
    public TransferResult transfer(TransferCommand command) {
        if (command.senderUserId().equals(command.receiverUserId())) {
            throw new InvalidTransferException("Usuario nao pode transferir para si mesmo");
        }

        BigDecimal amount = Money.parse(command.amount());
        WalletEntity senderWallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(command.senderUserId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira de origem nao encontrada"));
        WalletEntity receiverWallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(command.receiverUserId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira de destino nao encontrada"));

        if (!WalletEntity.DEFAULT_CURRENCY.equals(senderWallet.getCurrency())
                || !WalletEntity.DEFAULT_CURRENCY.equals(receiverWallet.getCurrency())) {
            throw new InvalidTransferException("Moeda da transferencia deve ser BRL");
        }

        if (senderWallet.getBalance().compareTo(amount) < 0) {
            throw new InsufficientBalanceException("Saldo insuficiente");
        }

        senderWallet.debit(amount);
        receiverWallet.credit(amount);

        TransactionEntity transaction = transactionRepository.saveAndFlush(TransactionEntity.completedTransfer(
                senderWallet,
                receiverWallet,
                amount,
                TransactionDescription.normalize(command.description()),
                clock.instant()
        ));

        return new TransferResult(
                transaction.getId(),
                transaction.getType(),
                transaction.getStatus(),
                Money.format(transaction.getAmount()),
                transaction.getCurrency(),
                senderWallet.getId(),
                receiverWallet.getId(),
                new TransferSenderWalletResult(
                        senderWallet.getId(),
                        Money.format(senderWallet.getBalance()),
                        senderWallet.getCurrency(),
                        senderWallet.getUpdatedAt()
                ),
                transaction.getCreatedAt(),
                transaction.getCompletedAt()
        );
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/CreateDepositUseCase.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.Money;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionDescription;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.repository.TransactionRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Clock;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class CreateDepositUseCase {

    private final WalletRepository walletRepository;
    private final TransactionRepository transactionRepository;
    private final Clock clock;

    public CreateDepositUseCase(
            WalletRepository walletRepository,
            TransactionRepository transactionRepository,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.walletRepository = walletRepository;
        this.transactionRepository = transactionRepository;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Transactional
    public DepositResult deposit(DepositCommand command) {
        BigDecimal amount = Money.parse(command.amount());
        String description = TransactionDescription.normalize(command.description());
        WalletEntity wallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(command.userId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira nao encontrada para o usuario autenticado"));

        wallet.credit(amount);
        TransactionEntity transaction = transactionRepository.saveAndFlush(
                TransactionEntity.completedDeposit(wallet, amount, description, clock.instant())
        );

        return new DepositResult(
                transaction.getId(),
                transaction.getType(),
                transaction.getStatus(),
                Money.format(transaction.getAmount()),
                transaction.getCurrency(),
                transaction.getDescription(),
                new DepositWalletResult(
                        wallet.getId(),
                        Money.format(wallet.getBalance()),
                        wallet.getCurrency(),
                        wallet.getUpdatedAt()
                ),
                transaction.getCreatedAt(),
                transaction.getCompletedAt()
        );
    }
}
```

O deposito nao confere a moeda: a constraint `ck_wallets_currency_brl` ja garante carteiras em BRL.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/DepositService.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import org.springframework.core.retry.RetryTemplate;
import org.springframework.dao.OptimisticLockingFailureException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
public class DepositService {

    private final RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate;
    private final CreateDepositUseCase createDepositUseCase;

    public DepositService(RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate, CreateDepositUseCase createDepositUseCase) {
        this.balanceRetryTemplate = balanceRetryTemplate;
        this.createDepositUseCase = createDepositUseCase;
    }

    public DepositResult deposit(DepositCommand command) {
        try {
            return balanceRetryTemplate.invoke(() -> createDepositUseCase.deposit(command));
        } catch (OptimisticLockingFailureException exception) {
            throw new BalanceConflictException("Operacao concorrente na carteira, tente novamente");
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Extrato e comprovante aceitam transacao sem origem**

Em `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/GetWalletStatementUseCase.java`, substituir o metodo `entry`:

```java
    private StatementEntryResult entry(UUID walletId, TransactionEntity transaction) {
        WalletEntity senderWallet = transaction.getSenderWallet();
        boolean outgoing = senderWallet != null && senderWallet.getId().equals(walletId);
        WalletEntity counterparty = outgoing ? transaction.getReceiverWallet() : senderWallet;

        return new StatementEntryResult(
                transaction.getId(),
                transaction.getType(),
                transaction.getStatus(),
                outgoing ? StatementDirection.OUT : StatementDirection.IN,
                money(transaction.getAmount()),
                transaction.getCurrency(),
                counterparty == null ? null : counterparty.getUser().getName(),
                transaction.getDescription(),
                transaction.getCreatedAt(),
                transaction.getCompletedAt()
        );
    }
```

A query `findStatementTransactions` nao muda. No Hibernate 7, `t.senderWallet.id` le a coluna `sender_wallet_id` sem join, entao depositos com origem nula aparecem. O teste de integracao do Step 9 confirma.

Em `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/transaction/service/GetReceiptUseCase.java`, adicionar `import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionType;` e substituir o trecho que vai da busca da transacao ate a checagem de status por:

```java
        TransactionEntity transaction = transactionRepository.findReceiptById(transactionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new TransactionNotFoundException("Transacao nao encontrada"));

        UserEntity sender = transaction.getSenderWallet() == null ? null : transaction.getSenderWallet().getUser();
        UserEntity receiver = transaction.getReceiverWallet().getUser();
        boolean participates = (sender != null && sender.getId().equals(authenticatedUserId))
                || receiver.getId().equals(authenticatedUserId);

        if (!participates) {
            throw new ReceiptAccessDeniedException("Usuario nao participa da transacao");
        }

        if (transaction.getType() != TransactionType.TRANSFER) {
            throw new ReceiptUnavailableException("Comprovante disponivel apenas para transferencias");
        }

        if (transaction.getStatus() != TransactionStatus.COMPLETED) {
            throw new ReceiptUnavailableException("Comprovante disponivel apenas para transacoes concluidas");
        }
```

O resto do metodo nao muda. Depois da checagem de tipo, `sender` nunca e nulo (constraint `ck_transactions_transfer_has_sender`).

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.controllers;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.dto.DepositRequestDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.dto.DepositResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.dto.DepositWalletResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.dto.StatementEntryResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.dto.StatementResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.dto.WalletBalanceResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.GetWalletBalanceUseCase;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.GetWalletStatementUseCase;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.StatementQuery;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.StatementResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.WalletBalanceResult;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import java.net.URI;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/wallets")
public class WalletController {

    private final GetWalletBalanceUseCase getWalletBalanceUseCase;
    private final GetWalletStatementUseCase getWalletStatementUseCase;
    private final DepositService depositService;

    public WalletController(
            GetWalletBalanceUseCase getWalletBalanceUseCase,
            GetWalletStatementUseCase getWalletStatementUseCase,
            DepositService depositService
    ) {
        this.getWalletBalanceUseCase = getWalletBalanceUseCase;
        this.getWalletStatementUseCase = getWalletStatementUseCase;
        this.depositService = depositService;
    }

    @GetMapping("/me/balance")
    ResponseEntity<WalletBalanceResponseDTO> getMyBalance(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt) {
        WalletBalanceResult result = getWalletBalanceUseCase.getBalance(UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()));

        return ResponseEntity.ok(new WalletBalanceResponseDTO(
                result.walletId(),
                result.balance(),
                result.currency(),
                result.updatedAt()
        ));
    }

    @GetMapping("/me/statement")
    ResponseEntity<StatementResponseDTO> getMyStatement(
            @AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String from,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String to,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String type,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "createdAt,desc") String sort
    ) {
        StatementResult result = getWalletStatementUseCase.getStatement(
                UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()),
                new StatementQuery(page, size, from, to, type, sort)
        );

        return ResponseEntity.ok(new StatementResponseDTO(
                result.page(),
                result.size(),
                result.totalElements(),
                result.totalPages(),
                result.items().stream()
                        .map(item -> new StatementEntryResponseDTO(
                                item.transactionId(),
                                item.type(),
                                item.status(),
                                item.direction(),
                                item.amount(),
                                item.currency(),
                                item.counterpartyName(),
                                item.description(),
                                item.createdAt(),
                                item.completedAt()
                        ))
                        .toList()
        ));
    }

    @PostMapping("/me/deposits")
    ResponseEntity<DepositResponseDTO> deposit(
            @AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt,
            @Valid @RequestBody DepositRequestDTO request
    ) {
        DepositResult result = depositService.deposit(new DepositCommand(
                UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()),
                request.amount(),
                request.description()
        ));

        return ResponseEntity
                .created(URI.create("/transactions/" + result.transactionId()))
                .body(toResponse(result));
    }

    private static DepositResponseDTO toResponse(DepositResult result) {
        return new DepositResponseDTO(
                result.transactionId(),
                result.type(),
                result.status(),
                result.amount(),
                result.currency(),
                result.description(),
                new DepositWalletResponseDTO(
                        result.wallet().id(),
                        result.wallet().balance(),
                        result.wallet().currency(),
                        result.wallet().updatedAt()
                ),
                result.createdAt(),
                result.completedAt()
        );
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.nullValue;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.header;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestUser;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.BalanceConflictException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositService;
import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

class DepositIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private DepositService depositService;

    @Autowired
    private TransferService transferService;

    @Test
    void shouldCreditOwnWalletAndRegisterDepositTransaction() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");

        mockMvc.perform(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                        .header("Authorization", bearer(user))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "amount": "150.00",
                                  "description": "Aporte inicial"
                                }
                                """))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.type").value("DEPOSIT"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("COMPLETED"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.amount").value("150.00"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.description").value("Aporte inicial"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.wallet.id").value(user.walletId().toString()))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.wallet.balance").value("250.00"));

        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("250.00");
        Map<String, Object> row = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
                "select type, sender_wallet_id, receiver_wallet_id from transactions"
        );
        assertThat(row.get("type")).isEqualTo("DEPOSIT");
        assertThat(row.get("sender_wallet_id")).isNull();
        assertThat(row.get("receiver_wallet_id")).isEqualTo(user.walletId());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectDepositWithoutJwt() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "amount": "150.00"
                                }
                                """))
                .andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"0", "-5.00", "abc", "10.123", "100000000000000000000"})
    void shouldRejectInvalidAmountWithoutChangingBalance(String amount) throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");

        mockMvc.perform(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                        .header("Authorization", bearer(user))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "amount": "%s"
                                }
                                """.formatted(amount)))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INVALID_AMOUNT"));

        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("100.00");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from transactions", Long.class)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectMissingAmountWithInvalidAmountCode() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        mockMvc.perform(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                        .header("Authorization", bearer(user))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("{}"))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INVALID_AMOUNT"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Valor e obrigatorio"));
    }

    @Test
    void databaseShouldRejectTransferWithoutSender() {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbcTemplate.update("""
                insert into transactions (id, sender_wallet_id, receiver_wallet_id, type, status, amount, currency, created_at)
                values (?, null, ?, 'TRANSFER', 'COMPLETED', 10.00, 'BRL', now())
                """, UUID.randomUUID(), user.walletId()))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void databaseShouldRejectDepositWithSender() {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        TestUser other = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbcTemplate.update("""
                insert into transactions (id, sender_wallet_id, receiver_wallet_id, type, status, amount, currency, created_at)
                values (?, ?, ?, 'DEPOSIT', 'COMPLETED', 10.00, 'BRL', now())
                """, UUID.randomUUID(), other.walletId(), user.walletId()))
                .isInstanceOf(DataIntegrityViolationException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldListDepositInStatementAndFilterByType() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        TestUser other = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "50.00");
        depositService.deposit(new DepositCommand(user.userId(), "150.00", "Aporte"));
        transferService.transfer(new TransferCommand(other.userId(), user.userId(), "20.00", null));

        mockMvc.perform(get("/wallets/me/statement").header("Authorization", bearer(user)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.totalElements").value(2));

        mockMvc.perform(get("/wallets/me/statement").param("type", "DEPOSIT").header("Authorization", bearer(user)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.totalElements").value(1))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].type").value("DEPOSIT"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].direction").value("IN"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].counterpartyName").value(nullValue()))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.items[0].amount").value("150.00"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRefuseReceiptForDeposit() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        String body = mockMvc.perform(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                        .header("Authorization", bearer(user))
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "amount": "10.00"
                                }
                                """))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().exists("Location"))
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String transactionId = JsonPath.read(body, "$.transactionId");

        mockMvc.perform(get("/transactions/{id}/receipt", transactionId).header("Authorization", bearer(user)))
                .andExpect(status().isConflict())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("RECEIPT_UNAVAILABLE"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Comprovante disponivel apenas para transferencias"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldSumConcurrentDepositsCorrectly() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        List<String> outcomes = runConcurrently(10, () -> depositAsClient(user, "10.00"));

        assertThat(outcomes).containsOnly("SUCCESS");
        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("100.00");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from transactions", Long.class)).isEqualTo(10L);
    }

    private String depositAsClient(TestUser user, String amount) {
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++) {
            try {
                depositService.deposit(new DepositCommand(user.userId(), amount, null));
                return "SUCCESS";
            } catch (BalanceConflictException exception) {
                // o cliente repete depois de 409 BALANCE_CONFLICT
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("Deposito nao terminou depois de 50 tentativas do cliente");
    }
}
```

Esperado: `BUILD SUCCESS` nos dois. Se `shouldListDepositInStatementAndFilterByType` nao encontrar o deposito, a query gerou inner join em `senderWallet`. Nesse caso, em `TransactionRepository.findStatementTransactions`, trocar `from TransactionEntity t where (t.senderWallet.id = :walletId or t.receiverWallet.id = :walletId)` por `from TransactionEntity t left join t.senderWallet sw where (sw.id = :walletId or t.receiverWallet.id = :walletId)` e rodar de novo.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Money`, `TransactionDescription`, `Clock`, `BusinessException`, `ErrorCode.IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_*`.
- Produces:
  - `enum IdempotentOperation { TRANSFER_CREATE, DEPOSIT_CREATE }`.
  - `IdempotencyKeys.HEADER = "Idempotency-Key"`, `IdempotencyKeys.REPLAYED_HEADER = "Idempotent-Replayed"`, `IdempotencyKeys.require(String rawKey): String`.
  - `RequestHash.of(String... fields): String` (SHA-256 em hexadecimal minusculo, campos unidos por `|`, `null` vira vazio).
  - `record IdempotencyRequest(UUID userId, IdempotentOperation operation, String key, String requestHash)`.
  - `record IdempotentResult<T>(T result, boolean replayed)`.
  - `IdempotencyService(JdbcClient, JsonMapper, Clock)` com `@Transactional(MANDATORY) <T> IdempotentResult<T> execute(IdempotencyRequest request, Class<T> resultType, Supplier<T> action)`.
  - `IdempotencyKeyCleanupJob(JdbcClient, Clock)` com `int purgeExpiredKeys()`.
  - `record TransferCommand(UUID senderUserId, UUID receiverUserId, String amount, String description, String idempotencyKey)`.
  - `record DepositCommand(UUID userId, String amount, String description, String idempotencyKey)`.
  - `CreateTransferUseCase(WalletRepository, TransactionRepository, Clock, IdempotencyService)` com `IdempotentResult<TransferResult> transfer(TransferCommand)`.
  - `CreateDepositUseCase(WalletRepository, TransactionRepository, Clock, IdempotencyService)` com `IdempotentResult<DepositResult> deposit(DepositCommand)`.
  - `TransferService.transfer(...)` e `DepositService.deposit(...)` passam a retornar `IdempotentResult<...>`.
  - `support/PassThroughIdempotencyService` (fake que so executa a acao e guarda `lastRequest`).

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/idempotency/RequestHashTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class RequestHashTest {

    @Test
    void shouldProduceSha256HexOfTheJoinedFields() {
        assertThat(RequestHash.of("abc"))
                .isEqualTo("ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldBeStableAndSensitiveToEveryField() {
        String hash = RequestHash.of("receiver", "10.00", "Almoco");

        assertThat(hash).hasSize(64).matches("[0-9a-f]{64}");
        assertThat(RequestHash.of("receiver", "10.00", "Almoco")).isEqualTo(hash);
        assertThat(RequestHash.of("receiver", "10.01", "Almoco")).isNotEqualTo(hash);
        assertThat(RequestHash.of("10.00", "receiver", "Almoco")).isNotEqualTo(hash);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldTreatNullAsEmptyString() {
        assertThat(RequestHash.of("10.00", null)).isEqualTo(RequestHash.of("10.00", ""));
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/idempotency/IdempotencyKeysTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;
import java.util.stream.Stream;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.MethodSource;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;

class IdempotencyKeysTest {

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"a", "0b9f3c1e-5a52-4c8b-9a51-1f3d2e7c0001", "chave_1-A"})
    void shouldAcceptValidKeys(String key) {
        assertThat(IdempotencyKeys.require(key)).isEqualTo(key);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldAcceptKeyWithOneHundredCharacters() {
        String key = "a".repeat(100);

        assertThat(IdempotencyKeys.require(key)).isEqualTo(key);
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @MethodSource("missingKeys")
    void shouldRequireKey(String key) {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> IdempotencyKeys.require(key))
                .isInstanceOfSatisfying(IdempotencyKeyRequiredException.class, exception ->
                        assertThat(exception.getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED))
                .hasMessage("Idempotency-Key e obrigatorio");
    }

    static Stream<String> missingKeys() {
        return Stream.of(null, "", "   ");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @MethodSource("invalidKeys")
    void shouldRejectInvalidKey(String key) {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> IdempotencyKeys.require(key))
                .isInstanceOfSatisfying(IdempotencyKeyInvalidException.class, exception ->
                        assertThat(exception.getErrorCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID));
    }

    static Stream<String> invalidKeys() {
        return Stream.of("chave com espaco", "chave!", "a".repeat(101), " chave");
    }
}
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id UUID NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    operation VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    request_hash CHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    response_status INTEGER,
    response_body JSONB,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_idempotency_keys_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT uk_idempotency_keys_user_operation_key UNIQUE (user_id, operation, idempotency_key)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_idempotency_keys_expires_at ON idempotency_keys (expires_at);
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency;

import java.util.regex.Pattern;

public final class IdempotencyKeys {

    public static final String HEADER = "Idempotency-Key";
    public static final String REPLAYED_HEADER = "Idempotent-Replayed";

    private static final Pattern VALID_KEY = Pattern.compile("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}$");

    private IdempotencyKeys() {
    }

    public static String require(String rawKey) {
        if (rawKey == null || rawKey.isBlank()) {
            throw new IdempotencyKeyRequiredException("Idempotency-Key e obrigatorio");
        }
        if (!VALID_KEY.matcher(rawKey).matches()) {
            throw new IdempotencyKeyInvalidException(
                    "Idempotency-Key deve ter de 1 a 100 caracteres entre A-Z, a-z, 0-9, _ e -"
            );
        }
        return rawKey;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/idempotency/RequestHash.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.HexFormat;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public final class RequestHash {

    private RequestHash() {
    }

    public static String of(String... fields) {
        String canonical = Arrays.stream(fields)
                .map(field -> field == null ? "" : field)
                .collect(Collectors.joining("|"));
        try {
            byte[] digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(canonical.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            return HexFormat.of().formatHex(digest);
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("SHA-256 indisponivel", exception);
        }
    }
}
```

A descricao e sempre o ultimo campo do hash, entao um `|` dentro dela nao gera ambiguidade.

- [ ] **Step 5: Servico de idempotencia, job e agendamento**

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/idempotency/IdempotencyService.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Supplier;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

@Service
public class IdempotencyService {

    static final Duration TTL = Duration.ofHours(24);
    private static final int CREATED = 201;

    private final JdbcClient jdbcClient;
    private final JsonMapper jsonMapper;
    private final Clock clock;

    public IdempotencyService(JdbcClient jdbcClient, JsonMapper jsonMapper, Clock clock) {
        this.jdbcClient = jdbcClient;
        this.jsonMapper = jsonMapper;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.MANDATORY)
    public <T> IdempotentResult<T> execute(IdempotencyRequest request, Class<T> resultType, Supplier<T> action) {
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        deleteIfExpired(request, now);

        if (claim(request, now)) {
            T result = action.get();
            storeResponse(request, result);
            return new IdempotentResult<>(result, false);
        }

        StoredResponse stored = load(request);
        if (!stored.requestHash().equals(request.requestHash())) {
            throw new IdempotencyKeyReusedException("Idempotency-Key ja utilizada com outro payload");
        }
        return new IdempotentResult<>(jsonMapper.readValue(stored.responseBody(), resultType), true);
    }

    private void deleteIfExpired(IdempotencyRequest request, Instant now) {
        jdbcClient.sql("""
                        delete from idempotency_keys
                        where user_id = :userId
                          and operation = :operation
                          and idempotency_key = :key
                          and expires_at < :now
                        """)
                .param("userId", request.userId())
                .param("operation", request.operation().name())
                .param("key", request.key())
                .param("now", utc(now))
                .update();
    }

    private boolean claim(IdempotencyRequest request, Instant now) {
        int inserted = jdbcClient.sql("""
                        insert into idempotency_keys (
                            id, user_id, idempotency_key, operation, request_hash, created_at, expires_at
                        ) values (
                            :id, :userId, :key, :operation, :requestHash, :createdAt, :expiresAt
                        )
                        on conflict (user_id, operation, idempotency_key) do nothing
                        """)
                .param("id", UUID.randomUUID())
                .param("userId", request.userId())
                .param("key", request.key())
                .param("operation", request.operation().name())
                .param("requestHash", request.requestHash())
                .param("createdAt", utc(now))
                .param("expiresAt", utc(now.plus(TTL)))
                .update();
        return inserted == 1;
    }

    private void storeResponse(IdempotencyRequest request, Object result) {
        jdbcClient.sql("""
                        update idempotency_keys
                        set response_status = :status,
                            response_body = cast(:body as jsonb)
                        where user_id = :userId
                          and operation = :operation
                          and idempotency_key = :key
                        """)
                .param("status", CREATED)
                .param("body", jsonMapper.writeValueAsString(result))
                .param("userId", request.userId())
                .param("operation", request.operation().name())
                .param("key", request.key())
                .update();
    }

    private StoredResponse load(IdempotencyRequest request) {
        return jdbcClient.sql("""
                        select request_hash, response_body::text as response_body
                        from idempotency_keys
                        where user_id = :userId
                          and operation = :operation
                          and idempotency_key = :key
                        """)
                .param("userId", request.userId())
                .param("operation", request.operation().name())
                .param("key", request.key())
                .query((resultSet, rowNumber) -> new StoredResponse(
                        resultSet.getString("request_hash"),
                        resultSet.getString("response_body")
                ))
                .single();
    }

    private static OffsetDateTime utc(Instant instant) {
        return instant.atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC);
    }

    private record StoredResponse(String requestHash, String responseBody) {
    }
}
```

Como funciona a concorrencia: o `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` de uma segunda requisicao com a mesma chave espera a transacao da primeira terminar. Se a primeira confirmar, o `INSERT` nao insere nada e o `select` seguinte (READ COMMITTED) ja enxerga a resposta salva. Se a primeira for desfeita, a segunda insere e executa. O `JdbcClient` usa a mesma conexao da transacao JPA aberta pelo use case.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/idempotency/IdempotencyKeyCleanupJob.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Component
public class IdempotencyKeyCleanupJob {

    static final int BATCH_SIZE = 1000;

    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(IdempotencyKeyCleanupJob.class);

    private final JdbcClient jdbcClient;
    private final Clock clock;

    public IdempotencyKeyCleanupJob(JdbcClient jdbcClient, Clock clock) {
        this.jdbcClient = jdbcClient;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 3 * * *", zone = "America/Sao_Paulo")
    public int purgeExpiredKeys() {
        int total = 0;
        int deleted;
        do {
            deleted = jdbcClient.sql("""
                            delete from idempotency_keys
                            where id in (
                                select id
                                from idempotency_keys
                                where expires_at < :now
                                order by expires_at
                                limit :limit
                            )
                            """)
                    .param("now", clock.instant().atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC))
                    .param("limit", BATCH_SIZE)
                    .update();
            total += deleted;
        } while (deleted == BATCH_SIZE);

        LOGGER.info("Chaves de idempotencia expiradas removidas: {}", total);
        return total;
    }
}
```

Cada lote roda em autocommit, sem transacao longa.

Substituir `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/transaction/service/CreateTransferUseCase.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyRequest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentOperation;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.RequestHash;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.Money;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionDescription;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.repository.TransactionRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.WalletNotFoundException;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class CreateTransferUseCase {

    private final WalletRepository walletRepository;
    private final TransactionRepository transactionRepository;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;

    public CreateTransferUseCase(
            WalletRepository walletRepository,
            TransactionRepository transactionRepository,
            Clock clock,
            IdempotencyService idempotencyService
    ) {
        this.walletRepository = walletRepository;
        this.transactionRepository = transactionRepository;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.idempotencyService = idempotencyService;
    }

    @Transactional
    public IdempotentResult<TransferResult> transfer(TransferCommand command) {
        if (command.senderUserId().equals(command.receiverUserId())) {
            throw new InvalidTransferException("Usuario nao pode transferir para si mesmo");
        }

        BigDecimal amount = Money.parse(command.amount());
        String description = TransactionDescription.normalize(command.description());
        IdempotencyRequest idempotencyRequest = new IdempotencyRequest(
                command.senderUserId(),
                IdempotentOperation.TRANSFER_CREATE,
                command.idempotencyKey(),
                RequestHash.of(command.receiverUserId().toString(), Money.format(amount), description)
        );

        return idempotencyService.execute(
                idempotencyRequest,
                TransferResult.class,
                () -> executeTransfer(command, amount, description)
        );
    }

    private TransferResult executeTransfer(TransferCommand command, BigDecimal amount, String description) {
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        WalletEntity senderWallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(command.senderUserId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira de origem nao encontrada"));
        WalletEntity receiverWallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(command.receiverUserId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira de destino nao encontrada"));

        if (!WalletEntity.DEFAULT_CURRENCY.equals(senderWallet.getCurrency())
                || !WalletEntity.DEFAULT_CURRENCY.equals(receiverWallet.getCurrency())) {
            throw new InvalidTransferException("Moeda da transferencia deve ser BRL");
        }

        if (senderWallet.getBalance().compareTo(amount) < 0) {
            throw new InsufficientBalanceException("Saldo insuficiente");
        }

        senderWallet.debit(amount);
        receiverWallet.credit(amount);

        TransactionEntity transaction = transactionRepository.saveAndFlush(TransactionEntity.completedTransfer(
                senderWallet,
                receiverWallet,
                amount,
                description,
                now
        ));

        return new TransferResult(
                transaction.getId(),
                transaction.getType(),
                transaction.getStatus(),
                Money.format(transaction.getAmount()),
                transaction.getCurrency(),
                senderWallet.getId(),
                receiverWallet.getId(),
                new TransferSenderWalletResult(
                        senderWallet.getId(),
                        Money.format(senderWallet.getBalance()),
                        senderWallet.getCurrency(),
                        senderWallet.getUpdatedAt()
                ),
                transaction.getCreatedAt(),
                transaction.getCompletedAt()
        );
    }
}
```

Validacoes de entrada (mesmo usuario, valor) rodam antes da chave, porque nao dependem de estado. Tudo o que depende de estado roda dentro de `execute`, na mesma transacao da chave.

Substituir `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/CreateDepositUseCase.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyRequest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentOperation;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.RequestHash;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.Money;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionDescription;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.repository.TransactionRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class CreateDepositUseCase {

    private final WalletRepository walletRepository;
    private final TransactionRepository transactionRepository;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;

    public CreateDepositUseCase(
            WalletRepository walletRepository,
            TransactionRepository transactionRepository,
            Clock clock,
            IdempotencyService idempotencyService
    ) {
        this.walletRepository = walletRepository;
        this.transactionRepository = transactionRepository;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.idempotencyService = idempotencyService;
    }

    @Transactional
    public IdempotentResult<DepositResult> deposit(DepositCommand command) {
        BigDecimal amount = Money.parse(command.amount());
        String description = TransactionDescription.normalize(command.description());
        IdempotencyRequest idempotencyRequest = new IdempotencyRequest(
                command.userId(),
                IdempotentOperation.DEPOSIT_CREATE,
                command.idempotencyKey(),
                RequestHash.of(Money.format(amount), description)
        );

        return idempotencyService.execute(
                idempotencyRequest,
                DepositResult.class,
                () -> executeDeposit(command, amount, description)
        );
    }

    private DepositResult executeDeposit(DepositCommand command, BigDecimal amount, String description) {
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        WalletEntity wallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(command.userId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira nao encontrada para o usuario autenticado"));

        wallet.credit(amount);
        TransactionEntity transaction = transactionRepository.saveAndFlush(
                TransactionEntity.completedDeposit(wallet, amount, description, now)
        );

        return new DepositResult(
                transaction.getId(),
                transaction.getType(),
                transaction.getStatus(),
                Money.format(transaction.getAmount()),
                transaction.getCurrency(),
                transaction.getDescription(),
                new DepositWalletResult(
                        wallet.getId(),
                        Money.format(wallet.getBalance()),
                        wallet.getCurrency(),
                        wallet.getUpdatedAt()
                ),
                transaction.getCreatedAt(),
                transaction.getCompletedAt()
        );
    }
}
```

Em `DepositService`: adicionar o mesmo import e trocar `public DepositResult deposit(DepositCommand command)` por `public IdempotentResult<DepositResult> deposit(DepositCommand command)`.

Substituir `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/transaction/controllers/TransactionController.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.controllers;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyKeys;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.dto.ReceiptResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.dto.TransferRequestDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.dto.TransferResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.dto.TransferSenderWalletResponseDTO;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.GetReceiptUseCase;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.ReceiptResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import java.net.URI;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/transactions")
public class TransactionController {

    private final TransferService transferService;
    private final GetReceiptUseCase getReceiptUseCase;

    public TransactionController(TransferService transferService, GetReceiptUseCase getReceiptUseCase) {
        this.transferService = transferService;
        this.getReceiptUseCase = getReceiptUseCase;
    }

    @PostMapping("/transfers")
    ResponseEntity<TransferResponseDTO> transfer(
            @AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt,
            @RequestHeader(name = IdempotencyKeys.HEADER, required = false) String idempotencyKey,
            @Valid @RequestBody TransferRequestDTO request
    ) {
        IdempotentResult<TransferResult> outcome = transferService.transfer(new TransferCommand(
                UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()),
                request.receiverUserId(),
                request.amount(),
                request.description(),
                IdempotencyKeys.require(idempotencyKey)
        ));
        TransferResult result = outcome.result();

        TransferResponseDTO response = new TransferResponseDTO(
                result.transactionId(),
                result.type(),
                result.status(),
                result.amount(),
                result.currency(),
                result.senderWalletId(),
                result.receiverWalletId(),
                new TransferSenderWalletResponseDTO(
                        result.senderWallet().id(),
                        result.senderWallet().balance(),
                        result.senderWallet().currency(),
                        result.senderWallet().updatedAt()
                ),
                result.createdAt(),
                result.completedAt()
        );

        ResponseEntity.BodyBuilder builder = ResponseEntity.created(URI.create("/transactions/" + result.transactionId()));
        if (outcome.replayed()) {
            builder.header(IdempotencyKeys.REPLAYED_HEADER, "true");
        }
        return builder.body(response);
    }

    @GetMapping("/{transactionId}/receipt")
    ResponseEntity<ReceiptResponseDTO> receipt(
            @AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt,
            @PathVariable UUID transactionId
    ) {
        ReceiptResult result = getReceiptUseCase.getReceipt(UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()), transactionId);

        return ResponseEntity.ok(new ReceiptResponseDTO(
                result.receiptId(),
                result.transactionId(),
                result.type(),
                result.status(),
                result.amount(),
                result.currency(),
                result.senderName(),
                result.senderDocumentMasked(),
                result.receiverName(),
                result.receiverDocumentMasked(),
                result.description(),
                result.createdAt(),
                result.completedAt()
        ));
    }
}
```

Em `WalletController`, adicionar os imports `ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyKeys`, `ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentResult` e `org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader`, e substituir o metodo `deposit` por:

```java
    @PostMapping("/me/deposits")
    ResponseEntity<DepositResponseDTO> deposit(
            @AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt,
            @RequestHeader(name = IdempotencyKeys.HEADER, required = false) String idempotencyKey,
            @Valid @RequestBody DepositRequestDTO request
    ) {
        IdempotentResult<DepositResult> outcome = depositService.deposit(new DepositCommand(
                UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()),
                request.amount(),
                request.description(),
                IdempotencyKeys.require(idempotencyKey)
        ));
        DepositResult result = outcome.result();

        ResponseEntity.BodyBuilder builder = ResponseEntity.created(URI.create("/transactions/" + result.transactionId()));
        if (outcome.replayed()) {
            builder.header(IdempotencyKeys.REPLAYED_HEADER, "true");
        }
        return builder.body(toResponse(result));
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/support/PassThroughIdempotencyService.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyRequest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotencyService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentResult;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class PassThroughIdempotencyService extends IdempotencyService {

    public IdempotencyRequest lastRequest;

    public PassThroughIdempotencyService() {
        super(null, null, null);
    }

    @Override
    public <T> IdempotentResult<T> execute(IdempotencyRequest request, Class<T> resultType, Supplier<T> action) {
        this.lastRequest = request;
        return new IdempotentResult<>(action.get(), false);
    }
}
```

```java
    @Test
    void shouldProtectTransferWithIdempotencyKeyAndNormalizedHash() {
        UUID senderUserId = UUID.randomUUID();
        UUID receiverUserId = UUID.randomUUID();
        FakeWalletRepository walletRepository = new FakeWalletRepository(Map.of(
                senderUserId, wallet(senderUserId, UUID.randomUUID(), "100.00"),
                receiverUserId, wallet(receiverUserId, UUID.randomUUID(), "10.00")
        ));
        PassThroughIdempotencyService idempotencyService = new PassThroughIdempotencyService();
        CreateTransferUseCase useCase = useCase(
                walletRepository.proxy(),
                new FakeTransactionRepository().proxy(),
                idempotencyService
        );

        useCase.transfer(new TransferCommand(senderUserId, receiverUserId, "25.5", " Almoco ", "chave-1"));

        assertThat(idempotencyService.lastRequest.userId()).isEqualTo(senderUserId);
        assertThat(idempotencyService.lastRequest.operation()).isEqualTo(IdempotentOperation.TRANSFER_CREATE);
        assertThat(idempotencyService.lastRequest.key()).isEqualTo("chave-1");
        assertThat(idempotencyService.lastRequest.requestHash())
                .isEqualTo(RequestHash.of(receiverUserId.toString(), "25.50", "Almoco"));
    }
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/common/idempotency/IdempotencyIntegrationTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.header;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestUser;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositService;
import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Stream;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.MethodSource;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockHttpServletRequestBuilder;

class IdempotencyIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private DepositService depositService;

    @Autowired
    private IdempotencyKeyCleanupJob cleanupJob;

    @Test
    void shouldRequireKeyOnDepositsAndTransfers() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");
        TestUser other = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        deposit(user, null, "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED"));
        transfer(user, other, null, "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED"));

        assertThat(count("transactions")).isZero();
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"", "   "})
    void shouldTreatBlankKeyAsMissing(String key) throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        deposit(user, key, "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED"));

        assertThat(count("idempotency_keys")).isZero();
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @MethodSource("invalidKeys")
    void shouldRejectInvalidKey(String key) throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        deposit(user, key, "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID"));
    }

    static Stream<String> invalidKeys() {
        return Stream.of("chave com espaco", "chave!", "a".repeat(101));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldReplaySameDepositAndExecuteItOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        String first = deposit(user, "deposito-1", "10.0")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().doesNotExist("Idempotent-Replayed"))
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String second = deposit(user, "deposito-1", "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().string("Idempotent-Replayed", "true"))
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();

        String firstId = JsonPath.read(first, "$.transactionId");
        String secondId = JsonPath.read(second, "$.transactionId");
        assertThat(secondId).isEqualTo(firstId);
        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("10.00");
        assertThat(count("transactions")).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select response_status from idempotency_keys", Integer.class))
                .isEqualTo(201);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldReplayTheOriginalSnapshotEvenAfterTheBalanceChanged() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        deposit(user, "deposito-1", "10.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        deposit(user, "deposito-2", "5.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());

        deposit(user, "deposito-1", "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().string("Idempotent-Replayed", "true"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.wallet.balance").value("10.00"));

        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("15.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldReplayTransferAndDebitOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "transferencia-1", "30.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        transfer(sender, receiver, "transferencia-1", "30.00")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().string("Idempotent-Replayed", "true"));

        assertThat(balanceOf(sender)).isEqualByComparingTo("70.00");
        assertThat(balanceOf(receiver)).isEqualByComparingTo("30.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectKeyReusedWithDifferentPayload() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        deposit(user, "deposito-1", "10.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());

        deposit(user, "deposito-1", "11.00")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Idempotency-Key ja utilizada com outro payload"));

        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("10.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldScopeKeysPerUser() throws Exception {
        TestUser joao = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        TestUser maria = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        deposit(joao, "mesma-chave", "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().doesNotExist("Idempotent-Replayed"));
        deposit(maria, "mesma-chave", "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().doesNotExist("Idempotent-Replayed"));

        assertThat(count("transactions")).isEqualTo(2L);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotKeepKeyWhenOperationIsRejected() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "transferencia-1", "50.00")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE"));
        assertThat(count("idempotency_keys")).isZero();

        deposit(sender, "deposito-1", "100.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        transfer(sender, receiver, "transferencia-1", "50.00")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().doesNotExist("Idempotent-Replayed"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldExecuteOnceUnderConcurrentRequestsWithTheSameKey() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        DepositCommand command = new DepositCommand(user.userId(), "10.00", null, "mesma-chave");

        List<IdempotentResult<DepositResult>> outcomes = runConcurrently(10, () -> depositService.deposit(command));

        assertThat(outcomes)
                .extracting(outcome -> outcome.result().transactionId())
                .containsOnly(outcomes.get(0).result().transactionId());
        assertThat(outcomes).filteredOn(outcome -> !outcome.replayed()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(count("transactions")).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("10.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldExecuteAgainAfterTheKeyExpired() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        deposit(user, "chave-expira", "10.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        jdbcTemplate.update("update idempotency_keys set expires_at = now() - interval '1 hour'");

        deposit(user, "chave-expira", "10.00")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(header().doesNotExist("Idempotent-Replayed"));

        assertThat(count("transactions")).isEqualTo(2L);
        assertThat(balanceOf(user)).isEqualByComparingTo("20.00");
    }

    @Test
    void cleanupJobShouldDeleteOnlyExpiredKeys() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        deposit(user, "chave-velha", "10.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        deposit(user, "chave-nova", "10.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        jdbcTemplate.update(
                "update idempotency_keys set expires_at = now() - interval '1 minute' where idempotency_key = ?",
                "chave-velha"
        );

        assertThat(cleanupJob.purgeExpiredKeys()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForList("select idempotency_key from idempotency_keys", String.class))
                .containsExactly("chave-nova");
    }

    private ResultActions deposit(TestUser user, String key, String amount) throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletRequestBuilder request = post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(user))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "amount": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(amount));
        if (key != null) {
            request.header("Idempotency-Key", key);
        }
        return mockMvc.perform(request);
    }

    private ResultActions transfer(TestUser sender, TestUser receiver, String key, String amount) throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletRequestBuilder request = post("/transactions/transfers")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(sender))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "receiverUserId": "%s",
                          "amount": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(receiver.userId(), amount));
        if (key != null) {
            request.header("Idempotency-Key", key);
        }
        return mockMvc.perform(request);
    }

    private long count(String table) {
        return jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from " + table, Long.class);
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/audit/AuditEntryTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class AuditEntryTest {

    @Test
    void shouldBuildMetadataSkippingNullsAndUsingEnumNames() {
        UUID walletId = UUID.randomUUID();

        Map<String, String> metadata = AuditEntry.metadata(
                "amount", "10.00",
                "walletId", walletId,
                "failureCode", ErrorCode.INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE,
                "description", null
        );

        assertThat(metadata).containsExactlyInAnyOrderEntriesOf(Map.of(
                "amount", "10.00",
                "walletId", walletId.toString(),
                "failureCode", "INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE"
        ));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldDeriveOutcomeFromFailureCode() {
        AuditEntry success = AuditEntry.success(AuditAction.DEPOSIT_CREATED, UUID.randomUUID(), Map.of());
        AuditEntry failure = AuditEntry.failure(AuditAction.DEPOSIT_REJECTED, null, ErrorCode.INVALID_AMOUNT, Map.of());

        assertThat(success.outcome()).isEqualTo(AuditOutcome.SUCCESS);
        assertThat(failure.outcome()).isEqualTo(AuditOutcome.FAILURE);
        assertThat(AuditAction.DEPOSIT_REJECTED.resourceType()).isEqualTo(AuditResourceType.WALLET);
        assertThat(AuditAction.TRANSFER_CREATED.resourceType()).isEqualTo(AuditResourceType.TRANSACTION);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldKeepActorWhenInformed() {
        UUID userId = UUID.randomUUID();

        AuditEntry entry = AuditEntry.success(AuditAction.LOGOUT, null, null).withActor(userId);

        assertThat(entry.actorUserId()).isEqualTo(userId);
        assertThat(entry.metadata()).isEmpty();
    }
}
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_events (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    actor_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    actor_user_id UUID,
    action VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
    resource_type VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    resource_id UUID,
    outcome VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    failure_code VARCHAR(60),
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    ip VARCHAR(45),
    request_id VARCHAR(64),
    CONSTRAINT ck_audit_events_outcome CHECK (outcome IN ('SUCCESS', 'FAILURE')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_audit_events_failure_code CHECK (outcome = 'FAILURE' OR failure_code IS NULL)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_events_actor_occurred ON audit_events (actor_user_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_events_resource ON audit_events (resource_type, resource_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_events_request_id ON audit_events (request_id);

CREATE FUNCTION prevent_audit_events_change() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit_events e append-only';
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_audit_events_append_only
    BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON audit_events
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_audit_events_change();

CREATE TABLE revinfo (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    timestamp BIGINT NOT NULL,
    user_id UUID,
    request_id VARCHAR(64)
);

CREATE TABLE users_aud (
    id UUID NOT NULL,
    rev BIGINT NOT NULL,
    revtype SMALLINT,
    name VARCHAR(120),
    email VARCHAR(254),
    document VARCHAR(14),
    email_verified BOOLEAN,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CONSTRAINT pk_users_aud PRIMARY KEY (id, rev),
    CONSTRAINT fk_users_aud_rev FOREIGN KEY (rev) REFERENCES revinfo (id)
);

CREATE TABLE wallets_aud (
    id UUID NOT NULL,
    rev BIGINT NOT NULL,
    revtype SMALLINT,
    user_id UUID,
    balance NUMERIC(19, 2),
    currency VARCHAR(3),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    version BIGINT,
    CONSTRAINT pk_wallets_aud PRIMARY KEY (id, rev),
    CONSTRAINT fk_wallets_aud_rev FOREIGN KEY (rev) REFERENCES revinfo (id)
);

CREATE TABLE transactions_aud (
    id UUID NOT NULL,
    rev BIGINT NOT NULL,
    revtype SMALLINT,
    sender_wallet_id UUID,
    receiver_wallet_id UUID,
    type VARCHAR(20),

    failure_reason VARCHAR(255),
    CONSTRAINT pk_transactions_aud PRIMARY KEY (id, rev),
    CONSTRAINT fk_transactions_aud_rev FOREIGN KEY (rev) REFERENCES revinfo (id)
);
```

```java
    TRANSFER_REJECTED(AuditResourceType.WALLET),
    DEPOSIT_CREATED(AuditResourceType.TRANSACTION),
    DEPOSIT_REJECTED(AuditResourceType.WALLET);
```
