`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/audit/AuditEntry.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public record AuditEntry(
        AuditAction action,
        UUID actorUserId,
        UUID resourceId,
        ErrorCode failureCode,
        Map<String, String> metadata
) {
    public AuditEntry {
        metadata = metadata == null ? Map.of() : Map.copyOf(metadata);
    }

    public static AuditEntry success(AuditAction action, UUID resourceId, Map<String, String> metadata) {
        return new AuditEntry(action, null, resourceId, null, metadata);
    }

    public static AuditEntry failure(
            AuditAction action,
            UUID resourceId,
            ErrorCode failureCode,
            Map<String, String> metadata
    ) {
        return new AuditEntry(action, null, resourceId, failureCode, metadata);
    }

    public AuditEntry withActor(UUID actorUserId) {
        return new AuditEntry(action, actorUserId, resourceId, failureCode, metadata);
    }

    public AuditOutcome outcome() {
        return failureCode == null ? AuditOutcome.SUCCESS : AuditOutcome.FAILURE;
    }

    public static Map<String, String> metadata(Object... keysAndValues) {
        if (keysAndValues.length % 2 != 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("metadata exige pares de chave e valor");
        }
        Map<String, String> metadata = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < keysAndValues.length; i += 2) {
            Object value = keysAndValues[i + 1];
            if (value != null) {
                metadata.put((String) keysAndValues[i], value instanceof Enum<?> constant ? constant.name() : value.toString());
            }
        }
        return metadata;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/audit/CurrentActor.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.server.resource.authentication.JwtAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.web.context.request.RequestAttributes;
import org.springframework.web.context.request.RequestContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.context.request.ServletRequestAttributes;

public final class CurrentActor {

    private CurrentActor() {
    }

    public static UUID userId() {
        Authentication authentication = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        if (authentication instanceof JwtAuthenticationToken jwtAuthentication) {
            try {
                return UUID.fromString(jwtAuthentication.getToken().getSubject());
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {
                return null;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static String remoteAddress() {
        RequestAttributes attributes = RequestContextHolder.getRequestAttributes();
        if (attributes instanceof ServletRequestAttributes servletAttributes) {
            return servletAttributes.getRequest().getRemoteAddr();
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

`X-Forwarded-For` nao e usado nesta fase, como define a spec.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/audit/AuditLogger.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.web.RequestIdFilter;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.slf4j.MDC;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

@Service
public class AuditLogger {

    private final JdbcClient jdbcClient;
    private final JsonMapper jsonMapper;
    private final Clock clock;

    public AuditLogger(JdbcClient jdbcClient, JsonMapper jsonMapper, Clock clock) {
        this.jdbcClient = jdbcClient;
        this.jsonMapper = jsonMapper;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.MANDATORY)
    public void record(AuditEntry entry) {
        insert(entry);
    }

    @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.REQUIRES_NEW)
    public void recordFailure(AuditEntry entry) {
        insert(entry);
    }

    private void insert(AuditEntry entry) {
        UUID actorUserId = entry.actorUserId() != null ? entry.actorUserId() : CurrentActor.userId();
        AuditActorType actorType = actorUserId != null ? AuditActorType.USER : AuditActorType.ANONYMOUS;

        jdbcClient.sql("""
                        insert into audit_events (
                            id, occurred_at, actor_type, actor_user_id, action, resource_type, resource_id,
                            outcome, failure_code, metadata, ip, request_id
                        ) values (
                            :id, :occurredAt, :actorType, :actorUserId, :action, :resourceType, :resourceId,
                            :outcome, :failureCode, cast(:metadata as jsonb), :ip, :requestId
                        )
                        """)
                .param("id", UUID.randomUUID())
                .param("occurredAt", clock.instant().atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC))
                .param("actorType", actorType.name())
                .param("actorUserId", actorUserId)
                .param("action", entry.action().name())
                .param("resourceType", entry.action().resourceType().name())
                .param("resourceId", entry.resourceId())
                .param("outcome", entry.outcome().name())
                .param("failureCode", entry.failureCode() == null ? null : entry.failureCode().name())
                .param("metadata", jsonMapper.writeValueAsString(entry.metadata()))
                .param("ip", CurrentActor.remoteAddress())
                .param("requestId", MDC.get(RequestIdFilter.MDC_KEY))
                .update();
    }
}
```

`record` exige transacao aberta: o evento de sucesso so existe se a operacao confirmar. `recordFailure` abre transacao propria: o rollback da operacao nao apaga a falha.

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditAction;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditEntry;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditLogger;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.domain.RefreshTokenEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.repository.RefreshTokenRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.BusinessException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.domain.UserEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.user.repository.UserRepository;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class AuthService {

    private static final Duration REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL = Duration.ofDays(7);

    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final RefreshTokenRepository refreshTokenRepository;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    private final AccessTokenService accessTokenService;
    private final AuditLogger auditLogger;

    public AuthService(
            UserRepository userRepository,
            RefreshTokenRepository refreshTokenRepository,
            PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder,
            AccessTokenService accessTokenService,
            AuditLogger auditLogger
    ) {
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
        this.refreshTokenRepository = refreshTokenRepository;
        this.passwordEncoder = passwordEncoder;
        this.accessTokenService = accessTokenService;
        this.auditLogger = auditLogger;
    }

    @Transactional
    public AuthTokenResult login(String email, String password) {
        String normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
        UserEntity user = userRepository.findByEmail(normalizedEmail).orElse(null);

        if (user == null || !passwordEncoder.matches(password, user.getPasswordHash())) {
            throw loginFailed(normalizedEmail, new InvalidCredentialsException("Credenciais invalidas"));
        }

        if (!user.isEmailVerified()) {
            throw loginFailed(normalizedEmail, new EmailNotVerifiedException("E-mail ainda nao verificado"));
        }

        AccessTokenResult accessToken = accessTokenService.issue(user);
        RefreshTokenEntity refreshToken = new RefreshTokenEntity(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                user,
                Instant.now().plus(REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL)
        );
        refreshTokenRepository.save(refreshToken);
        auditLogger.record(AuditEntry.success(AuditAction.LOGIN_SUCCEEDED, null, Map.of()).withActor(user.getId()));

        return new AuthTokenResult(
                accessToken.accessToken(),
                accessToken.tokenType(),
                accessToken.expiresIn(),
                refreshToken.getToken()
        );
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AccessTokenResult refresh(String token) {
        RefreshTokenEntity refreshToken = findValidRefreshToken(token);
        return accessTokenService.issue(refreshToken.getUser());
    }

    @Transactional
    public void logout(String token) {
        RefreshTokenEntity refreshToken = refreshTokenRepository.findByToken(token)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new InvalidRefreshTokenException("Refresh token invalido"));
        refreshToken.invalidate(Instant.now());
        auditLogger.record(AuditEntry.success(AuditAction.LOGOUT, null, Map.of())
                .withActor(refreshToken.getUser().getId()));
    }

    private BusinessException loginFailed(String email, BusinessException exception) {
        auditLogger.recordFailure(AuditEntry.failure(
                AuditAction.LOGIN_FAILED,
                null,
                exception.getErrorCode(),
                AuditEntry.metadata("email", email, "reason", exception.getErrorCode())
        ));
        return exception;
    }

    private RefreshTokenEntity findValidRefreshToken(String token) {
        RefreshTokenEntity refreshToken = refreshTokenRepository.findByToken(token)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new InvalidRefreshTokenException("Refresh token invalido"));

        Instant now = Instant.now();
        if (refreshToken.isInvalidated() || refreshToken.isExpired(now)) {
            throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException("Refresh token invalido");
        }

        return refreshToken;
    }
}
```

Substituir `src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/transaction/service/TransferService.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditAction;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditEntry;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditLogger;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.BusinessException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentResult;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.BalanceConflictException;
import java.util.Map;
import org.springframework.core.retry.RetryTemplate;
import org.springframework.dao.OptimisticLockingFailureException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
public class TransferService {

    private final RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate;
    private final CreateTransferUseCase createTransferUseCase;
    private final AuditLogger auditLogger;

    public TransferService(
            RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate,
            CreateTransferUseCase createTransferUseCase,
            AuditLogger auditLogger
    ) {
        this.balanceRetryTemplate = balanceRetryTemplate;
        this.createTransferUseCase = createTransferUseCase;
        this.auditLogger = auditLogger;
    }

    public IdempotentResult<TransferResult> transfer(TransferCommand command) {
        try {
            return transferWithRetry(command);
        } catch (BusinessException exception) {
            auditLogger.recordFailure(AuditEntry.failure(
                    AuditAction.TRANSFER_REJECTED,
                    null,
                    exception.getErrorCode(),
                    rejectionMetadata(command, exception)
            ).withActor(command.senderUserId()));
            throw exception;
        }
    }

    private IdempotentResult<TransferResult> transferWithRetry(TransferCommand command) {
        try {
            return balanceRetryTemplate.invoke(() -> createTransferUseCase.transfer(command));
        } catch (OptimisticLockingFailureException exception) {
            throw new BalanceConflictException("Operacao concorrente na carteira, tente novamente");
        }
    }

    private static Map<String, String> rejectionMetadata(TransferCommand command, BusinessException exception) {
        return AuditEntry.metadata(
                "amount", command.amount(),
                "receiverUserId", command.receiverUserId(),
                "failureCode", exception.getErrorCode()
        );
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditAction;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditEntry;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditLogger;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.BusinessException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.idempotency.IdempotentResult;
import java.util.Map;
import org.springframework.core.retry.RetryTemplate;
import org.springframework.dao.OptimisticLockingFailureException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
public class DepositService {

    private final RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate;
    private final CreateDepositUseCase createDepositUseCase;
    private final AuditLogger auditLogger;

    public DepositService(
            RetryTemplate balanceRetryTemplate,
            CreateDepositUseCase createDepositUseCase,
            AuditLogger auditLogger
    ) {
        this.balanceRetryTemplate = balanceRetryTemplate;
        this.createDepositUseCase = createDepositUseCase;
        this.auditLogger = auditLogger;
    }

    public IdempotentResult<DepositResult> deposit(DepositCommand command) {
        try {
            return depositWithRetry(command);
        } catch (BusinessException exception) {
            auditLogger.recordFailure(AuditEntry.failure(
                    AuditAction.DEPOSIT_REJECTED,
                    null,
                    exception.getErrorCode(),
                    rejectionMetadata(command, exception)
            ).withActor(command.userId()));
            throw exception;
        }
    }

    private IdempotentResult<DepositResult> depositWithRetry(DepositCommand command) {
        try {
            return balanceRetryTemplate.invoke(() -> createDepositUseCase.deposit(command));
        } catch (OptimisticLockingFailureException exception) {
            throw new BalanceConflictException("Operacao concorrente na carteira, tente novamente");
        }
    }

    private static Map<String, String> rejectionMetadata(DepositCommand command, BusinessException exception) {
        return AuditEntry.metadata(
                "amount", command.amount(),
                "failureCode", exception.getErrorCode()
        );
    }
}
```

A falha e registrada uma unica vez, depois da ultima tentativa: e o motivo de usar `RetryTemplate` em vez de `@Retryable` (spec 10).

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditEntry;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditLogger;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RecordingAuditLogger extends AuditLogger {

    public final List<AuditEntry> recorded = new ArrayList<>();
    public final List<AuditEntry> failures = new ArrayList<>();

    public RecordingAuditLogger() {
        super(null, null, null);
    }

    @Override
    public void record(AuditEntry entry) {
        recorded.add(entry);
    }

    @Override
    public void recordFailure(AuditEntry entry) {
        failures.add(entry);
    }
}
```

    @Test
    void shouldAuditTheRejectionOnceAfterTheLastAttempt() {
        RecordingAuditLogger auditLogger = new RecordingAuditLogger();
        FakeCreateTransferUseCase useCase = new FakeCreateTransferUseCase(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        TransferService service = new TransferService(new ResilienceConfig().balanceRetryTemplate(), useCase, auditLogger);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.transfer(COMMAND)).isInstanceOf(BalanceConflictException.class);

        assertThat(useCase.calls).isEqualTo(4);
        assertThat(auditLogger.failures).singleElement().satisfies(entry -> {
            assertThat(entry.action()).isEqualTo(AuditAction.TRANSFER_REJECTED);
            assertThat(entry.failureCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.BALANCE_CONFLICT);
            assertThat(entry.actorUserId()).isEqualTo(COMMAND.senderUserId());
            assertThat(entry.metadata())
                    .containsEntry("amount", "10.00")
                    .containsEntry("receiverUserId", COMMAND.receiverUserId().toString())
                    .containsEntry("failureCode", "BALANCE_CONFLICT");
        });
    }

    @Test
    void shouldAuditSuccessfulLogin() {

        AuthService authService = new AuthService(

                new FakeRefreshTokenRepository().proxy(),

    @Test

            assertThat(entry.action()).isEqualTo(AuditAction.LOGIN_FAILED);
            assertThat(entry.failureCode()).isEqualTo(ErrorCode.INVALID_CREDENTIALS);
            assertThat(entry.metadata())
                    .containsEntry("email", "joao@email.com")
                    .containsEntry("reason", "INVALID_CREDENTIALS")
                    .doesNotContainValue("errada");
        });
    }

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.EmailVerificationSender;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class CapturingEmailVerificationSender implements EmailVerificationSender {

    private final Map<String, String> lastTokenByEmail = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Override
    public void sendVerification(String email, String token) {
        lastTokenByEmail.put(email, token);
    }

    public String lastTokenFor(String email) {
        return lastTokenByEmail.get(email);
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/support/CapturingPasswordRecoverySender.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.auth.service.PasswordRecoverySender;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public class CapturingPasswordRecoverySender implements PasswordRecoverySender {

    private final Map<String, String> lastTokenByEmail = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Override
    public void sendPasswordRecovery(String email, String token) {
        lastTokenByEmail.put(email, token);
    }

    public String lastTokenFor(String email) {
        return lastTokenByEmail.get(email);
    }
}

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.CapturingEmailVerificationSender;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.CapturingPasswordRecoverySender;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestUser;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.DepositService;
import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class AuditIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private CapturingEmailVerificationSender emailVerificationSender;

    @Autowired
    private CapturingPasswordRecoverySender passwordRecoverySender;

    @Autowired
    private DepositService depositService;

    @Test
    void shouldRecordTransferCreatedWithRequestContext() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        String body = transfer(sender, receiver.userId(), "10.00", "req-audit-1")
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String transactionId = JsonPath.read(body, "$.transactionId");

        Map<String, Object> event = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
                "select * from audit_events where action = 'TRANSFER_CREATED'"
        );
        assertThat(event.get("outcome")).isEqualTo("SUCCESS");
        assertThat(event.get("actor_type")).isEqualTo("USER");
        assertThat(event.get("actor_user_id")).isEqualTo(sender.userId());
        assertThat(event.get("resource_type")).isEqualTo("TRANSACTION");
        assertThat(event.get("resource_id")).isEqualTo(UUID.fromString(transactionId));
        assertThat(event.get("failure_code")).isNull();
        assertThat(event.get("request_id")).isEqualTo("req-audit-1");
        assertThat(event.get("ip")).isEqualTo("127.0.0.1");
        assertThat(metadataValue("TRANSFER_CREATED", "amount")).isEqualTo("10.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRecordRejectedTransferEvenAfterRollback() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        transfer(sender, receiver.userId(), "50.00", "req-audit-2")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE"));

        Map<String, Object> event = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
                "select * from audit_events where action = 'TRANSFER_REJECTED'"
        );
        assertThat(event.get("outcome")).isEqualTo("FAILURE");
        assertThat(event.get("failure_code")).isEqualTo("INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE");
        assertThat(event.get("resource_type")).isEqualTo("WALLET");
        assertThat(event.get("actor_user_id")).isEqualTo(sender.userId());
        assertThat(event.get("request_id")).isEqualTo("req-audit-2");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from transactions", Long.class)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRecordRejectionForUnknownReceiverWithoutKeepingTheIdempotencyKey() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");

        transfer(sender, UUID.randomUUID(), "10.00", "req-audit-3")
                .andExpect(status().isNotFound())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("WALLET_NOT_FOUND"));

        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select failure_code from audit_events where action = 'TRANSFER_REJECTED'", String.class
        )).isEqualTo("WALLET_NOT_FOUND");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from idempotency_keys", Long.class)).isZero();
        assertThat(balanceOf(sender)).isEqualByComparingTo("100.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRecordDepositCreatedAndRejected() throws Exception {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        deposit(user, "15.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        deposit(user, "0").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());

        assertThat(metadataValue("DEPOSIT_CREATED", "walletId")).isEqualTo(user.walletId().toString());
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select failure_code from audit_events where action = 'DEPOSIT_REJECTED'", String.class
        )).isEqualTo("INVALID_AMOUNT");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRecordTheAuthenticationLifecycleWithoutSensitiveData() throws Exception {
        String email = "joao.audit@email.com";
        String document = "52998224725";

        mockMvc.perform(post("/users")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "name": "Joao Silva",
                                  "email": "%s",
                                  "password": "%s",
                                  "document": "%s"
                                }
                                """.formatted(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD, document)))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated());
        login(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD).andExpect(status().isForbidden());

        String verificationToken = emailVerificationSender.lastTokenFor(email);
        mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/email-verification/confirm")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "token": "%s"
                                }
                                """.formatted(verificationToken)))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());

        login(email, "SenhaErrada1").andExpect(status().isUnauthorized());
        String loginBody = login(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD)
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        String accessToken = JsonPath.read(loginBody, "$.accessToken");
        String refreshToken = JsonPath.read(loginBody, "$.refreshToken");

        mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/logout")
                        .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + accessToken)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "refreshToken": "%s"
                                }
                                """.formatted(refreshToken)))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());

        requestPasswordRecovery(email);
        requestPasswordRecovery("ninguem@email.com");
        String resetToken = passwordRecoverySender.lastTokenFor(email);
        mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/password-recovery/reset")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "token": "%s",
                                  "newPassword": "NovaSenha123"
                                }
                                """.formatted(resetToken)))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());

        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForList("select action from audit_events", String.class))
                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(
                        "USER_REGISTERED",
                        "LOGIN_FAILED",
                        "EMAIL_VERIFIED",
                        "LOGIN_FAILED",
                        "LOGIN_SUCCEEDED",
                        "LOGOUT",
                        "PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED",
                        "PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED"
                );
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
                "select metadata->>'reason' from audit_events where action = 'LOGIN_FAILED'", String.class
        )).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED", "INVALID_CREDENTIALS");

        String allMetadata = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select string_agg(metadata::text, ' ') from audit_events", String.class
        );
        assertThat(allMetadata).doesNotContain(
                DEFAULT_PASSWORD,
                "SenhaErrada1",
                "NovaSenha123",
                document,
                verificationToken,
                resetToken,
                refreshToken,
                accessToken
        );
    }

    @Test
    void auditEventsShouldBeAppendOnly() {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        depositService.deposit(new DepositCommand(user.userId(), "10.00", null, "append-only-1"));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbcTemplate.update("update audit_events set action = 'ALTERADO'"))
                .hasStackTraceContaining("append-only");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> jdbcTemplate.update("delete from audit_events"))
                .hasStackTraceContaining("append-only");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from audit_events", Long.class)).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    private ResultActions transfer(TestUser sender, UUID receiverUserId, String amount, String requestId) throws Exception {
        return mockMvc.perform(post("/transactions/transfers")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(sender))
                .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                .header("X-Request-Id", requestId)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "receiverUserId": "%s",
                          "amount": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(receiverUserId, amount)));
    }

    private ResultActions deposit(TestUser user, String amount) throws Exception {
        return mockMvc.perform(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(user))
                .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "amount": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(amount)));
    }

    private ResultActions login(String email, String password) throws Exception {
        return mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/login")
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "email": "%s",
                          "password": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(email, password)));
    }

    private void requestPasswordRecovery(String email) throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/password-recovery/request")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "email": "%s"
                                }
                                """.formatted(email)))
                .andExpect(status().isNoContent());
    }

    private String metadataValue(String action, String key) {
        return jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select metadata ->> cast(? as text) from audit_events where action = ?",
                String.class,
                key,
                action
        );
    }
}
```

package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.BalanceConflictException;
import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

class EnversIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private TransferService transferService;

    @Test
    void shouldRecordTransferRevisionWithUserAndRequestId() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        String body = mockMvc.perform(post("/transactions/transfers")
                        .header("Authorization", bearer(sender))
                        .header("Idempotency-Key", "envers-1")
                        .header("X-Request-Id", "req-envers-1")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {
                                  "receiverUserId": "%s",
                                  "amount": "10.00"
                                }
                                """.formatted(receiver.userId())))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        UUID transactionId = UUID.fromString(JsonPath.read(body, "$.transactionId"));

        Long revision = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select rev from transactions_aud where id = ?", Long.class, transactionId
        );
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
                "select id from wallets_aud where rev = ? and revtype = 1", UUID.class, revision
        )).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(sender.walletId(), receiver.walletId());
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select balance from wallets_aud where rev = ? and id = ?", BigDecimal.class, revision, sender.walletId()
        )).isEqualByComparingTo("90.00");

        Map<String, Object> revisionInfo = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
                "select user_id, request_id, \"timestamp\" as revision_timestamp from revinfo where id = ?", revision
        );
        assertThat(revisionInfo.get("user_id")).isEqualTo(sender.userId());
        assertThat(revisionInfo.get("request_id")).isEqualTo("req-envers-1");
        assertThat((Long) revisionInfo.get("revision_timestamp")).isPositive();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotStorePasswordHashInUserHistory() {
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
                "select column_name from information_schema.columns where table_name = 'users_aud'", String.class
        )).contains("email", "document").doesNotContain("password_hash");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select count(*) from users_aud where id = ?", Long.class, user.userId()
        )).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldWriteOneRevisionPerCommittedTransferEvenWithRetries() throws Exception {
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        List<Boolean> outcomes = runConcurrently(10, () -> transferAsClient(sender, receiver));

        assertThat(outcomes).containsOnly(true);
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select count(*) from wallets_aud where id = ?", Long.class, sender.walletId()
        )).isEqualTo(11L);
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select max(version) from wallets_aud where id = ?", Long.class, sender.walletId()
        )).isEqualTo(10L);
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("select count(*) from transactions_aud", Long.class)).isEqualTo(10L);
    }

    private boolean transferAsClient(TestUser sender, TestUser receiver) {
        String idempotencyKey = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++) {
            try {
                transferService.transfer(new TransferCommand(sender.userId(), receiver.userId(), "10.00", null, idempotencyKey));
                return true;
            } catch (BalanceConflictException exception) {
                // o cliente repete depois de 409 BALANCE_CONFLICT
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}
```

A carteira do remetente tem 11 linhas em `wallets_aud`: a criacao e as 10 transferencias confirmadas. Tentativas desfeitas pelo retry nao geram linha.

- [ ] **Step 3: Dependencia e configuracao**

Em `pom.xml`, depois de `spring-boot-starter-data-jpa`:

```xml
		<dependency>
			<groupId>org.hibernate.orm</groupId>
			<artifactId>hibernate-envers</artifactId>
		</dependency>
```

A versao (7.4.1.Final) vem do `spring-boot-starter-parent`.

Em `src/main/resources/application.properties`, acrescentar:

```properties
spring.jpa.properties.hibernate.envers.audit_table_suffix=_aud
spring.jpa.properties.hibernate.envers.revision_field_name=rev
spring.jpa.properties.hibernate.envers.revision_type_field_name=revtype
spring.jpa.properties.hibernate.envers.do_not_audit_optimistic_locking_field=false
```

A ultima linha faz o Envers gravar `version` em `wallets_aud` e `wallet_limits_aud`; por padrao ele ignora o campo `@Version`.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/audit/AuditRevisionEntity.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.GeneratedValue;
import jakarta.persistence.GenerationType;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.hibernate.envers.RevisionEntity;
import org.hibernate.envers.RevisionNumber;
import org.hibernate.envers.RevisionTimestamp;

@Entity
@Table(name = "revinfo")
@RevisionEntity(AuditRevisionListener.class)
public class AuditRevisionEntity {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @RevisionNumber
    private Long id;

    @RevisionTimestamp
    @Column(name = "timestamp", nullable = false)
    private long timestamp;

    @Column(name = "user_id")
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(name = "request_id", length = 64)
    private String requestId;

    public Long getId() {
        return id;
    }

    public long getTimestamp() {
        return timestamp;
    }

    public UUID getUserId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void setUserId(UUID userId) {
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public String getRequestId() {
        return requestId;
    }

    public void setRequestId(String requestId) {
        this.requestId = requestId;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/audit/AuditRevisionListener.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.web.RequestIdFilter;
import org.hibernate.envers.RevisionListener;
import org.slf4j.MDC;

public class AuditRevisionListener implements RevisionListener {

    @Override
    public void newRevision(Object revisionEntity) {
        AuditRevisionEntity revision = (AuditRevisionEntity) revisionEntity;
        revision.setUserId(CurrentActor.userId());
        revision.setRequestId(MDC.get(RequestIdFilter.MDC_KEY));
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.Optional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;

class LimitPeriodsTest {

    private static final ZoneId SAO_PAULO = ZoneId.of("America/Sao_Paulo");
    private static final LocalTime NIGHT_START = LocalTime.of(20, 0);
    private static final LocalTime NIGHT_END = LocalTime.of(6, 0);

    @Test
    void dayStartsAtLocalMidnightEvenWhenTheUtcDateIsAhead() {
        Instant now = Instant.parse("2026-10-15T01:00:00Z");

        LimitPeriods.Window day = LimitPeriods.day(now, SAO_PAULO);

        assertThat(day.start()).isEqualTo(Instant.parse("2026-10-14T03:00:00Z"));
        assertThat(day.end()).isEqualTo(now);
    }

    @Test
    void monthStartsOnTheLocalFirstDay() {
        Instant now = Instant.parse("2026-11-01T01:00:00Z");

        assertThat(LimitPeriods.month(now, SAO_PAULO).start()).isEqualTo(Instant.parse("2026-10-01T03:00:00Z"));
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"2026-10-15T20:00", "2026-10-16T05:59", "2026-10-15T23:00", "2026-10-16T02:00"})
    void nightWindowIsActive(String localDateTime) {
        assertThat(LimitPeriods.night(saoPaulo(localDateTime), SAO_PAULO, NIGHT_START, NIGHT_END)).isPresent();
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"2026-10-15T06:00", "2026-10-15T19:59", "2026-10-15T12:00"})
    void nightWindowIsInactive(String localDateTime) {
        assertThat(LimitPeriods.night(saoPaulo(localDateTime), SAO_PAULO, NIGHT_START, NIGHT_END)).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void windowAtElevenPmRunsUntilTomorrowMorning() {
        Optional<LimitPeriods.Window> window =
                LimitPeriods.night(saoPaulo("2026-10-15T23:00"), SAO_PAULO, NIGHT_START, NIGHT_END);

        assertThat(window).contains(new LimitPeriods.Window(saoPaulo("2026-10-15T20:00"), saoPaulo("2026-10-16T06:00")));
    }

    @Test
    void windowAtTwoAmStartedYesterdayEvening() {
        Optional<LimitPeriods.Window> window =
                LimitPeriods.night(saoPaulo("2026-10-16T02:00"), SAO_PAULO, NIGHT_START, NIGHT_END);

        assertThat(window).contains(new LimitPeriods.Window(saoPaulo("2026-10-15T20:00"), saoPaulo("2026-10-16T06:00")));
    }

    @Test
    void supportsWindowThatDoesNotCrossMidnight() {
        LocalTime start = LocalTime.of(1, 0);
        LocalTime end = LocalTime.of(5, 0);

        assertThat(LimitPeriods.night(saoPaulo("2026-10-15T03:00"), SAO_PAULO, start, end)).isPresent();
        assertThat(LimitPeriods.night(saoPaulo("2026-10-15T05:00"), SAO_PAULO, start, end)).isEmpty();
    }

    private static Instant saoPaulo(String localDateTime) {
        return LocalDateTime.parse(localDateTime).atZone(SAO_PAULO).toInstant();
    }
}
```

package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.config;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class LimitsPropertiesTest {

    @Test
    void shouldExposeParsedTimezoneAndNightWindow() {
        LimitsProperties properties = new LimitsProperties(
                "America/Sao_Paulo",
                new LimitsProperties.Night("20:00", "06:00"),
                transfer("5000.00", "10000.00", "50000.00", "1000.00", "1000.00"),
                new LimitsProperties.Deposit(new BigDecimal("10000.00"), new BigDecimal("20000.00"))
        );

        assertThat(properties.zoneId()).isEqualTo(ZoneId.of("America/Sao_Paulo"));
        assertThat(properties.night().startTime()).isEqualTo(LocalTime.of(20, 0));
        assertThat(properties.night().endTime()).isEqualTo(LocalTime.of(6, 0));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectIncoherentTransferLimits() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> transfer("5000.00", "4000.00", "50000.00", "1000.00", "1000.00"))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("transferDaily");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> transfer("5000.00", "10000.00", "50000.00", "6000.00", "7000.00"))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("transferNightPerOperation");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectDepositPerOperationAboveDaily() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new LimitsProperties.Deposit(new BigDecimal("30000.00"), new BigDecimal("20000.00")))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectUnknownTimezoneAndInvalidNightTime() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new LimitsProperties.Night("25:00", "06:00"))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new LimitsProperties(
                "Lua/Base",
                new LimitsProperties.Night("20:00", "06:00"),
                transfer("5000.00", "10000.00", "50000.00", "1000.00", "1000.00"),
                new LimitsProperties.Deposit(new BigDecimal("10000.00"), new BigDecimal("20000.00"))
        )).isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class);
    }

    private static LimitsProperties.Transfer transfer(
            String perOperation,
            String daily,
            String monthly,
            String nightPerOperation,
            String nightWindow
    ) {
        return new LimitsProperties.Transfer(
                new BigDecimal(perOperation),
                new BigDecimal(daily),
                new BigDecimal(monthly),
                new BigDecimal(nightPerOperation),
                new BigDecimal(nightWindow)
        );
    }
}

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/EffectiveTransferLimits.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.config.LimitsProperties;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public record EffectiveTransferLimits(
        BigDecimal perOperation,
        BigDecimal daily,
        BigDecimal monthly,
        BigDecimal nightPerOperation,
        BigDecimal nightWindow
) {
    public static EffectiveTransferLimits of(
            LimitsProperties.Transfer global,
            BigDecimal customPerOperation,
            BigDecimal customDaily,
            BigDecimal customMonthly,
            BigDecimal customNightPerOperation,
            BigDecimal customNightWindow
    ) {
        return new EffectiveTransferLimits(
                min(global.perOperation(), customPerOperation),
                min(global.daily(), customDaily),
                min(global.monthly(), customMonthly),
                min(global.nightPerOperation(), customNightPerOperation),
                min(global.nightWindow(), customNightWindow)
        );
    }

    public Map<String, String> coherenceViolations() {
        Map<String, String> violations = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        if (perOperation.compareTo(daily) > 0) {
            violations.put("transferDaily", "Limite diario deve ser maior ou igual ao limite por operacao");
        }
        if (daily.compareTo(monthly) > 0) {
            violations.put("transferMonthly", "Limite mensal deve ser maior ou igual ao limite diario");
        }
        if (nightPerOperation.compareTo(nightWindow) > 0) {
            violations.put(
                    "transferNightWindow",
                    "Limite da janela noturna deve ser maior ou igual ao limite noturno por operacao"
            );
        }
        if (nightPerOperation.compareTo(perOperation) > 0) {
            violations.put(
                    "transferNightPerOperation",
                    "Limite noturno por operacao deve ser menor ou igual ao limite por operacao"
            );
        }
        return violations;
    }

    private static BigDecimal min(BigDecimal global, BigDecimal custom) {
        return custom == null ? global : global.min(custom);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/config/LimitsProperties.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.config;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.EffectiveTransferLimits;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Positive;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.DateTimeException;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException;
import java.util.Map;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;

@Validated
@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "walletpay.limits")
public record LimitsProperties(
        @NotBlank String timezone,
        @NotNull @Valid Night night,
        @NotNull @Valid Transfer transfer,
        @NotNull @Valid Deposit deposit
) {
    public LimitsProperties {
        if (timezone != null) {
            try {
                ZoneId.of(timezone);
            } catch (DateTimeException exception) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("walletpay.limits.timezone invalido: " + timezone, exception);
            }
        }
    }

    public ZoneId zoneId() {
        return ZoneId.of(timezone);
    }

    public record Night(@NotBlank String start, @NotBlank String end) {

        public Night {
            requireTime(start, "start");
            requireTime(end, "end");
        }

        public LocalTime startTime() {
            return LocalTime.parse(start);
        }

        public LocalTime endTime() {
            return LocalTime.parse(end);
        }

        private static void requireTime(String value, String field) {
            if (value == null) {
                return;
            }
            try {
                LocalTime.parse(value);
            } catch (DateTimeParseException exception) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        "walletpay.limits.night." + field + " deve estar no formato HH:mm", exception
                );
            }
        }
    }

    public record Transfer(
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal perOperation,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal daily,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal monthly,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal nightPerOperation,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal nightWindow
    ) {
        public Transfer {
            if (perOperation != null && daily != null && monthly != null
                    && nightPerOperation != null && nightWindow != null) {
                Map<String, String> violations = new EffectiveTransferLimits(
                        perOperation, daily, monthly, nightPerOperation, nightWindow
                ).coherenceViolations();
                if (!violations.isEmpty()) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("walletpay.limits.transfer incoerente: " + violations);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public record Deposit(
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal perOperation,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal daily
    ) {
        public Deposit {
            if (perOperation != null && daily != null && perOperation.compareTo(daily) > 0) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        "walletpay.limits.deposit: per-operation deve ser menor ou igual a daily"
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`@Validated` cobre ausencia e valores nao positivos; os construtores cobrem fuso, horario e coerencia. A aplicacao nao sobe com configuracao invalida.

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.Optional;

public final class LimitPeriods {

    public record Window(Instant start, Instant end) {
    }

    private LimitPeriods() {
    }

    public static Window day(Instant now, ZoneId zone) {
        Instant start = now.atZone(zone).toLocalDate().atStartOfDay(zone).toInstant();
        return new Window(start, now);
    }

    public static Window month(Instant now, ZoneId zone) {
        Instant start = now.atZone(zone).toLocalDate().withDayOfMonth(1).atStartOfDay(zone).toInstant();
        return new Window(start, now);
    }

    public static Optional<Window> night(Instant now, ZoneId zone, LocalTime start, LocalTime end) {
        LocalDateTime local = LocalDateTime.ofInstant(now, zone);
        LocalDate today = local.toLocalDate();
        LocalTime time = local.toLocalTime();

        if (start.isAfter(end)) {
            if (!time.isBefore(start)) {
                return Optional.of(window(today.atTime(start), today.plusDays(1).atTime(end), zone));
            }
            if (time.isBefore(end)) {
                return Optional.of(window(today.minusDays(1).atTime(start), today.atTime(end), zone));
            }
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        if (!time.isBefore(start) && time.isBefore(end)) {
            return Optional.of(window(today.atTime(start), today.atTime(end), zone));
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    private static Window window(LocalDateTime start, LocalDateTime end, ZoneId zone) {
        return new Window(start.atZone(zone).toInstant(), end.atZone(zone).toInstant());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/domain/WalletLimitEntity.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import jakarta.persistence.Version;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.hibernate.envers.Audited;

@Entity
@Audited
@Table(name = "wallet_limits")
public class WalletLimitEntity {

    @Id
    @Column(name = "wallet_id", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private UUID walletId;

    @Column(name = "transfer_per_operation", precision = 19, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal transferPerOperation;

    @Column(name = "transfer_daily", precision = 19, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal transferDaily;

    @Column(name = "transfer_monthly", precision = 19, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal transferMonthly;

    @Column(name = "transfer_night_per_operation", precision = 19, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal transferNightPerOperation;

    @Column(name = "transfer_night_window", precision = 19, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal transferNightWindow;

    @Version
    @Column(nullable = false)
    private Long version;

    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant updatedAt;

    protected WalletLimitEntity() {
    }

    public WalletLimitEntity(UUID walletId) {
        this.walletId = walletId;
    }

    public void update(
            BigDecimal perOperation,
            BigDecimal daily,
            BigDecimal monthly,
            BigDecimal nightPerOperation,
            BigDecimal nightWindow,
            Instant now
    ) {
        this.transferPerOperation = perOperation;
        this.transferDaily = daily;
        this.transferMonthly = monthly;
        this.transferNightPerOperation = nightPerOperation;
        this.transferNightWindow = nightWindow;
        this.updatedAt = now;
    }

    public UUID getWalletId() {
        return walletId;
    }

    public BigDecimal getTransferPerOperation() {
        return transferPerOperation;
    }

    public BigDecimal getTransferDaily() {
        return transferDaily;
    }

    public BigDecimal getTransferMonthly() {
        return transferMonthly;
    }

    public BigDecimal getTransferNightPerOperation() {
        return transferNightPerOperation;
    }

    public BigDecimal getTransferNightWindow() {
        return transferNightWindow;
    }

    public Instant getUpdatedAt() {
        return updatedAt;
    }
}
```

`version` e `Long`: com id atribuido, o Spring Data usa `version == null` para saber que a entidade e nova e chama `persist` em vez de `merge`.

package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

public enum LimitType {
    PER_OPERATION,
    DAILY,
    MONTHLY,
    NIGHT_PER_OPERATION,
    NIGHT_WINDOW
}

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/LimitExceededException.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.BusinessException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.Money;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class LimitExceededException extends BusinessException {

    private final LimitType limitType;
    private final BigDecimal limit;
    private final BigDecimal available;

    public LimitExceededException(LimitType limitType, String message, BigDecimal limit, BigDecimal available) {
        super(ErrorCode.LIMIT_EXCEEDED, message);
        this.limitType = limitType;
        this.limit = limit;
        this.available = available;
    }

    public LimitType getLimitType() {
        return limitType;
    }

    @Override
    public Map<String, String> getFields() {
        Map<String, String> fields = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        fields.put("limitType", limitType.name());
        fields.put("limit", Money.format(limit));
        fields.put("available", Money.format(available));
        return fields;
    }
}

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/OperationLimitService.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionStatus;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.domain.TransactionType;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.repository.TransactionRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.config.LimitsProperties;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletLimitEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletLimitRepository;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Function;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
public class OperationLimitService {

    private final LimitsProperties properties;
    private final WalletLimitRepository walletLimitRepository;
    private final TransactionRepository transactionRepository;

    public OperationLimitService(
            LimitsProperties properties,
            WalletLimitRepository walletLimitRepository,
            TransactionRepository transactionRepository
    ) {
        this.properties = properties;
        this.walletLimitRepository = walletLimitRepository;
        this.transactionRepository = transactionRepository;
    }

    public EffectiveTransferLimits transferLimits(UUID walletId) {
        return effectiveTransferLimits(walletLimitRepository.findById(walletId).orElse(null));
    }

    public void checkTransfer(UUID walletId, BigDecimal amount, Instant now) {
        EffectiveTransferLimits limits = transferLimits(walletId);
        Optional<LimitPeriods.Window> night = nightWindow(now);

        requireWithinOperation(amount, limits.perOperation(), LimitType.PER_OPERATION,
                "Limite por operacao de transferencia excedido");
        if (night.isPresent()) {
            requireWithinOperation(amount, limits.nightPerOperation(), LimitType.NIGHT_PER_OPERATION,
                    "Limite noturno por operacao de transferencia excedido");
        }
        requireWithinPeriod(amount, limits.daily(),
                sentTransfers(walletId, LimitPeriods.day(now, zone()).start(), now),
                LimitType.DAILY, "Limite diario de transferencia excedido");
        requireWithinPeriod(amount, limits.monthly(),
                sentTransfers(walletId, LimitPeriods.month(now, zone()).start(), now),
                LimitType.MONTHLY, "Limite mensal de transferencia excedido");
        if (night.isPresent()) {
            requireWithinPeriod(amount, limits.nightWindow(),
                    sentTransfers(walletId, night.get().start(), now),
                    LimitType.NIGHT_WINDOW, "Limite noturno de transferencia excedido");
        }
    }

    public void checkDeposit(UUID walletId, BigDecimal amount, Instant now) {
        LimitsProperties.Deposit limits = properties.deposit();

        requireWithinOperation(amount, limits.perOperation(), LimitType.PER_OPERATION,
                "Limite por operacao de deposito excedido");
        requireWithinPeriod(amount, limits.daily(),
                receivedDeposits(walletId, LimitPeriods.day(now, zone()).start(), now),
                LimitType.DAILY, "Limite diario de deposito excedido");
    }

    public BigDecimal sentTransfers(UUID walletId, Instant from, Instant to) {
        return orZero(transactionRepository.sumOutgoingAmount(
                walletId, TransactionType.TRANSFER, TransactionStatus.COMPLETED, from, to
        ));
    }

    public BigDecimal receivedDeposits(UUID walletId, Instant from, Instant to) {
        return orZero(transactionRepository.sumIncomingAmount(
                walletId, TransactionType.DEPOSIT, TransactionStatus.COMPLETED, from, to
        ));
    }

    public Optional<LimitPeriods.Window> nightWindow(Instant now) {
        return LimitPeriods.night(now, zone(), properties.night().startTime(), properties.night().endTime());
    }

    private EffectiveTransferLimits effectiveTransferLimits(WalletLimitEntity custom) {
        return EffectiveTransferLimits.of(
                properties.transfer(),
                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferPerOperation),
                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferDaily),
                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferMonthly),
                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferNightPerOperation),
                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferNightWindow)
        );
    }

    private ZoneId zone() {
        return properties.zoneId();
    }

    private static BigDecimal customValue(WalletLimitEntity custom, Function<WalletLimitEntity, BigDecimal> getter) {
        return custom == null ? null : getter.apply(custom);
    }

    private static void requireWithinOperation(BigDecimal amount, BigDecimal limit, LimitType type, String message) {
        if (amount.compareTo(limit) > 0) {
            throw new LimitExceededException(type, message, limit, limit);
        }
    }

    private static void requireWithinPeriod(
            BigDecimal amount,
            BigDecimal limit,
            BigDecimal used,
            LimitType type,
            String message
    ) {
        if (used.add(amount).compareTo(limit) > 0) {
            throw new LimitExceededException(type, message, limit, limit.subtract(used).max(BigDecimal.ZERO));
        }
    }

    private static BigDecimal orZero(BigDecimal value) {
        return value == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : value;
    }
}
```

A ordem dos checks segue a spec 14: por operacao, noturno por operacao, diario, mensal, janela noturna. A validacao nao precisa de lock proprio: duas operacoes na mesma carteira entram em conflito de versao, e a retentativa recalcula o valor utilizado.

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/support/MutableClock.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

public class MutableClock extends Clock {

    private final AtomicReference<Instant> fixedInstant = new AtomicReference<>();

    public void setInstant(Instant instant) {
        fixedInstant.set(instant);
    }

    public void reset() {
        fixedInstant.set(null);
    }

    @Override
    public ZoneId getZone() {
        return ZoneOffset.UTC;
    }

    @Override
    public Clock withZone(ZoneId zone) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("MutableClock usa sempre UTC");
    }

    @Override
    public Instant instant() {
        Instant instant = fixedInstant.get();
        return instant != null ? instant : Instant.now();
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/LimitsIntegrationTest.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestUser;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferCommand;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.transaction.service.TransferService;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletLimitEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletLimitRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.BalanceConflictException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.LimitExceededException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.LimitPeriods;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service.OperationLimitService;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class LimitsIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    private static final ZoneId SAO_PAULO = ZoneId.of("America/Sao_Paulo");

    @Autowired
    private OperationLimitService operationLimitService;

    @Autowired
    private WalletLimitRepository walletLimitRepository;

    @Autowired
    private TransferService transferService;

    @Test
    void shouldRejectTransferAbovePerOperationLimit() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "5000.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("LIMIT_EXCEEDED"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Limite por operacao de transferencia excedido"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("PER_OPERATION"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limit").value("5000.00"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.available").value("5000.00"));
        assertThat(balanceOf(sender)).isEqualByComparingTo("20000.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectTransferAboveDailyLimit() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "5000.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        transfer(sender, receiver, "5000.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        transfer(sender, receiver, "0.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Limite diario de transferencia excedido"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("DAILY"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limit").value("10000.00"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.available").value("0.00"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectTransferAboveMonthlyLimitUsingTheSaoPauloCalendar() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");
        insertTransfer(sender, receiver, "46000.00", Instant.parse("2026-10-02T15:00:00Z"), "COMPLETED");
        insertTransfer(sender, receiver, "5000.00", saoPaulo("2026-09-30T23:00"), "COMPLETED");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "4000.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("MONTHLY"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limit").value("50000.00"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.available").value("4000.00"));
        transfer(sender, receiver, "4000.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldApplyNightPerOperationLimit() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T23:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "1000.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Limite noturno por operacao de transferencia excedido"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("NIGHT_PER_OPERATION"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limit").value("1000.00"));
        transfer(sender, receiver, "1000.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldCountTransfersFromYesterdayEveningInTheNightWindow() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-16T02:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");
        insertTransfer(sender, receiver, "900.00", saoPaulo("2026-10-15T21:00"), "COMPLETED");
        insertTransfer(sender, receiver, "500.00", saoPaulo("2026-10-15T19:00"), "COMPLETED");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "100.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        transfer(sender, receiver, "0.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Limite noturno de transferencia excedido"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("NIGHT_WINDOW"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.available").value("0.00"));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldApplyCustomLimitLowerThanTheGlobalOne() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");
        transactionTemplate.executeWithoutResult(status -> {
            WalletLimitEntity limits = new WalletLimitEntity(sender.walletId());
            limits.update(new BigDecimal("2000.00"), null, null, null, null, Instant.now());
            walletLimitRepository.save(limits);
        });

        transfer(sender, receiver, "2000.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("PER_OPERATION"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limit").value("2000.00"));
        transfer(sender, receiver, "2000.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldApplyGlobalDepositLimits() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser user = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");

        deposit(user, "10000.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Limite por operacao de deposito excedido"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("PER_OPERATION"));
        deposit(user, "10000.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        deposit(user, "10000.00").andExpect(status().isCreated());
        deposit(user, "0.01")
                .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Limite diario de deposito excedido"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.limitType").value("DAILY"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields.available").value("0.00"));
    }

    @Test
    void usageShouldIgnoreFailedTransactionsOtherTypesAndOtherWallets() {
        Instant now = useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "0.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");
        TestUser other = createVerifiedUser("Ana Lima", "0.00");
        Instant oneHourAgo = now.minus(Duration.ofHours(1));
        insertTransfer(sender, receiver, "3000.00", oneHourAgo, "FAILED");
        insertDeposit(sender, "3000.00", oneHourAgo);
        insertTransfer(other, receiver, "3000.00", oneHourAgo, "COMPLETED");
        insertTransfer(sender, receiver, "100.00", oneHourAgo, "COMPLETED");
        Instant dayStart = LimitPeriods.day(now, SAO_PAULO).start();

        assertThat(operationLimitService.sentTransfers(sender.walletId(), dayStart, now)).isEqualByComparingTo("100.00");
        assertThat(operationLimitService.receivedDeposits(sender.walletId(), dayStart, now)).isEqualByComparingTo("3000.00");
    }

    @Test
    void concurrentTransfersMustNotExceedTheDailyLimitTogether() throws Exception {
        Instant now = useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");
        insertTransfer(sender, receiver, "4000.00", now.minus(Duration.ofHours(1)), "COMPLETED");

        List<String> outcomes = runConcurrently(2, () -> transferAsClient(sender, receiver, "5000.00"));

        assertThat(outcomes).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("SUCCESS", "LIMIT_EXCEEDED");
        assertThat(balanceOf(sender)).isEqualByComparingTo("15000.00");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldAuditLimitRejectionWithTheLimitType() throws Exception {
        useSaoPauloTime("2026-10-15T15:00");
        TestUser sender = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "20000.00");
        TestUser receiver = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");

        transfer(sender, receiver, "5000.01").andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity());

        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select failure_code from audit_events where action = 'TRANSFER_REJECTED'", String.class
        )).isEqualTo("LIMIT_EXCEEDED");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select metadata->>'limitType' from audit_events where action = 'TRANSFER_REJECTED'", String.class
        )).isEqualTo("PER_OPERATION");
    }

    private String transferAsClient(TestUser sender, TestUser receiver, String amount) {
        String idempotencyKey = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++) {
            try {
                transferService.transfer(new TransferCommand(sender.userId(), receiver.userId(), amount, null, idempotencyKey));
                return "SUCCESS";
            } catch (LimitExceededException exception) {
                return "LIMIT_EXCEEDED";
            } catch (BalanceConflictException exception) {
                // o cliente repete depois de 409 BALANCE_CONFLICT
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("Transferencia nao terminou depois de 50 tentativas do cliente");
    }

    private ResultActions transfer(TestUser sender, TestUser receiver, String amount) throws Exception {
        return mockMvc.perform(post("/transactions/transfers")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(sender))
                .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "receiverUserId": "%s",
                          "amount": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(receiver.userId(), amount)));
    }

    private ResultActions deposit(TestUser user, String amount) throws Exception {
        return mockMvc.perform(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(user))
                .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "amount": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(amount)));
    }

    private void insertTransfer(TestUser sender, TestUser receiver, String amount, Instant createdAt, String status) {
        jdbcTemplate.update("""
                insert into transactions (
                    id, sender_wallet_id, receiver_wallet_id, type, status, amount, currency, created_at, completed_at
                ) values (?, ?, ?, 'TRANSFER', ?, ?, 'BRL', ?, ?)
                """,
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                sender.walletId(),
                receiver.walletId(),
                status,
                new BigDecimal(amount),
                createdAt.atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC),
                createdAt.atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC)
        );
    }

    private void insertDeposit(TestUser user, String amount, Instant createdAt) {
        jdbcTemplate.update("""
                insert into transactions (
                    id, sender_wallet_id, receiver_wallet_id, type, status, amount, currency, created_at, completed_at
                ) values (?, null, ?, 'DEPOSIT', 'COMPLETED', ?, 'BRL', ?, ?)
                """,
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                user.walletId(),
                new BigDecimal(amount),
                createdAt.atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC),
                createdAt.atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC)
        );
    }

    private static Instant saoPaulo(String localDateTime) {
        return LocalDateTime.parse(localDateTime).atZone(SAO_PAULO).toInstant();
    }
}
```

As transacoes historicas sao inseridas direto no banco para montar o cenario de periodo sem mexer em saldo. A de `2026-09-30T23:00` em Sao Paulo e `2026-10-01T02:00Z`: se o mes fosse calculado em UTC, ela entraria em outubro e `available` seria `0.00`.

}

Se o driver reclamar, trocar `metadata ? 'previous.transferPerOperation'` por `jsonb_exists(metadata, 'previous.transferPerOperation')`.
