```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

public record WalletLimitsResult(
        TransferLimits transfer,
        DepositLimits deposit,
        Night night
) {
    public record LimitValue(String effective, String global, String custom) {
    }

    public record PeriodLimitValue(String effective, String global, String custom, String used, String available) {
    }

    public record TransferLimits(
            LimitValue perOperation,
            PeriodLimitValue daily,
            PeriodLimitValue monthly,
            LimitValue nightPerOperation,
            PeriodLimitValue nightWindow
    ) {
    }

    public record DepositLimits(LimitValue perOperation, PeriodLimitValue daily) {
    }

    public record Night(String start, String end, String timezone, boolean active) {
    }
}
```

Em `OperationLimitService`, adicionar `import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.Money;` e os metodos:

```java
    public WalletLimitsResult describe(UUID walletId, Instant now) {
        WalletLimitEntity custom = walletLimitRepository.findById(walletId).orElse(null);
        LimitsProperties.Transfer global = properties.transfer();
        LimitsProperties.Deposit deposit = properties.deposit();
        EffectiveTransferLimits effective = effectiveTransferLimits(custom);
        Optional<LimitPeriods.Window> night = nightWindow(now);
        Instant dayStart = LimitPeriods.day(now, zone()).start();
        Instant monthStart = LimitPeriods.month(now, zone()).start();

        BigDecimal dailyUsed = sentTransfers(walletId, dayStart, now);
        BigDecimal monthlyUsed = sentTransfers(walletId, monthStart, now);
        BigDecimal nightUsed = night
                .map(window -> sentTransfers(walletId, window.start(), now))
                .orElse(BigDecimal.ZERO);
        BigDecimal depositDailyUsed = receivedDeposits(walletId, dayStart, now);

        return new WalletLimitsResult(
                new WalletLimitsResult.TransferLimits(
                        limitValue(effective.perOperation(), global.perOperation(),
                                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferPerOperation)),
                        periodValue(effective.daily(), global.daily(),
                                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferDaily), dailyUsed),
                        periodValue(effective.monthly(), global.monthly(),
                                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferMonthly), monthlyUsed),
                        limitValue(effective.nightPerOperation(), global.nightPerOperation(),
                                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferNightPerOperation)),
                        periodValue(effective.nightWindow(), global.nightWindow(),
                                customValue(custom, WalletLimitEntity::getTransferNightWindow), nightUsed)
                ),
                new WalletLimitsResult.DepositLimits(
                        limitValue(deposit.perOperation(), deposit.perOperation(), null),
                        periodValue(deposit.daily(), deposit.daily(), null, depositDailyUsed)
                ),
                new WalletLimitsResult.Night(
                        properties.night().start(),
                        properties.night().end(),
                        properties.timezone(),
                        night.isPresent()
                )
        );
    }

    private static WalletLimitsResult.LimitValue limitValue(BigDecimal effective, BigDecimal global, BigDecimal custom) {
        return new WalletLimitsResult.LimitValue(
                Money.format(effective),
                Money.format(global),
                custom == null ? null : Money.format(custom)
        );
    }

    private static WalletLimitsResult.PeriodLimitValue periodValue(
            BigDecimal effective,
            BigDecimal global,
            BigDecimal custom,
            BigDecimal used
    ) {
        return new WalletLimitsResult.PeriodLimitValue(
                Money.format(effective),
                Money.format(global),
                custom == null ? null : Money.format(custom),
                Money.format(used),
                Money.format(effective.subtract(used).max(BigDecimal.ZERO))
        );
    }
```

Fora da janela noturna, `nightWindow.used` e `0.00` e `night.active` e `false`; `available` nunca fica negativo.

`src/main/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/wallet/service/GetWalletLimitsUseCase.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class GetWalletLimitsUseCase {

    private final WalletRepository walletRepository;
    private final OperationLimitService operationLimitService;
    private final Clock clock;

    public GetWalletLimitsUseCase(
            WalletRepository walletRepository,
            OperationLimitService operationLimitService,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.walletRepository = walletRepository;
        this.operationLimitService = operationLimitService;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public WalletLimitsResult getLimits(UUID userId) {
        WalletEntity wallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(userId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira nao encontrada para o usuario autenticado"));
        return operationLimitService.describe(wallet.getId(), clock.instant());
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.BusinessException;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.error.ErrorCode;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class InvalidLimitsException extends BusinessException {

    private final Map<String, String> fields;

    public InvalidLimitsException(String message, Map<String, String> fields) {
        super(ErrorCode.INVALID_LIMITS, message);
        this.fields = new LinkedHashMap<>(fields);
    }

    @Override
    public Map<String, String> getFields() {
        return fields;
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.service;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditAction;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditEntry;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.audit.AuditLogger;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.common.money.Money;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.config.LimitsProperties;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.domain.WalletLimitEntity;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletLimitRepository;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.wallet.repository.WalletRepository;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class UpdateWalletLimitsUseCase {

    private final WalletRepository walletRepository;
    private final WalletLimitRepository walletLimitRepository;
    private final OperationLimitService operationLimitService;
    private final LimitsProperties properties;
    private final AuditLogger auditLogger;
    private final Clock clock;

    public UpdateWalletLimitsUseCase(
            WalletRepository walletRepository,
            WalletLimitRepository walletLimitRepository,
            OperationLimitService operationLimitService,
            LimitsProperties properties,
            AuditLogger auditLogger,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.walletRepository = walletRepository;
        this.walletLimitRepository = walletLimitRepository;
        this.operationLimitService = operationLimitService;
        this.properties = properties;
        this.auditLogger = auditLogger;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Transactional
    public WalletLimitsResult update(UpdateWalletLimitsCommand command) {
        WalletEntity wallet = walletRepository.findByUserId(command.userId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new WalletNotFoundException("Carteira nao encontrada para o usuario autenticado"));

        BigDecimal perOperation = parseOptional(command.transferPerOperation());
        BigDecimal daily = parseOptional(command.transferDaily());
        BigDecimal monthly = parseOptional(command.transferMonthly());
        BigDecimal nightPerOperation = parseOptional(command.transferNightPerOperation());
        BigDecimal nightWindow = parseOptional(command.transferNightWindow());

        LimitsProperties.Transfer global = properties.transfer();
        Map<String, String> errors = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        requireWithinGlobal(errors, "transferPerOperation", perOperation, global.perOperation());
        requireWithinGlobal(errors, "transferDaily", daily, global.daily());
        requireWithinGlobal(errors, "transferMonthly", monthly, global.monthly());
        requireWithinGlobal(errors, "transferNightPerOperation", nightPerOperation, global.nightPerOperation());
        requireWithinGlobal(errors, "transferNightWindow", nightWindow, global.nightWindow());
        if (errors.isEmpty()) {
            errors.putAll(EffectiveTransferLimits
                    .of(global, perOperation, daily, monthly, nightPerOperation, nightWindow)
                    .coherenceViolations());
        }
        if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
            throw new InvalidLimitsException("Limites invalidos", errors);
        }

        WalletLimitEntity limits = walletLimitRepository.findById(wallet.getId())
                .orElseGet(() -> new WalletLimitEntity(wallet.getId()));
        Map<String, String> metadata = new LinkedHashMap<>(snapshot("previous", limits));
        limits.update(perOperation, daily, monthly, nightPerOperation, nightWindow, clock.instant());
        walletLimitRepository.saveAndFlush(limits);
        metadata.putAll(snapshot("new", limits));

        auditLogger.record(AuditEntry.success(AuditAction.LIMITS_UPDATED, wallet.getId(), metadata)
                .withActor(command.userId()));
        return operationLimitService.describe(wallet.getId(), clock.instant());
    }

    private static BigDecimal parseOptional(String raw) {
        return raw == null ? null : Money.parse(raw);
    }

    private static void requireWithinGlobal(
            Map<String, String> errors,
            String field,
            BigDecimal value,
            BigDecimal global
    ) {
        if (value != null && value.compareTo(global) > 0) {
            errors.put(field, "Limite acima do teto global de " + Money.format(global));
        }
    }

    private static Map<String, String> snapshot(String prefix, WalletLimitEntity limits) {
        return AuditEntry.metadata(
                prefix + ".transferPerOperation", format(limits.getTransferPerOperation()),
                prefix + ".transferDaily", format(limits.getTransferDaily()),
                prefix + ".transferMonthly", format(limits.getTransferMonthly()),
                prefix + ".transferNightPerOperation", format(limits.getTransferNightPerOperation()),
                prefix + ".transferNightWindow", format(limits.getTransferNightWindow())
        );
    }

    private static String format(BigDecimal value) {
        return value == null ? null : Money.format(value);
    }
}
```

Campo ausente ou `null` remove o limite proprio. Campo `""` nao e "remover": cai em `Money.parse` e responde 400 `INVALID_AMOUNT`. No metadata, limite ausente significa "usa o global".

Em `audit/AuditAction.java`, substituir `DEPOSIT_REJECTED(AuditResourceType.WALLET);` por:

```java
    DEPOSIT_REJECTED(AuditResourceType.WALLET),
    LIMITS_UPDATED(AuditResourceType.WALLET);
```

```java
    @GetMapping("/me/limits")
    ResponseEntity<WalletLimitsResponseDTO> getMyLimits(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt) {
        WalletLimitsResult result = getWalletLimitsUseCase.getLimits(UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()));
        return ResponseEntity.ok(toResponse(result));
    }

    @PutMapping("/me/limits")
    ResponseEntity<WalletLimitsResponseDTO> updateMyLimits(
            @AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt,
            @RequestBody UpdateWalletLimitsRequestDTO request
    ) {
        WalletLimitsResult result = updateWalletLimitsUseCase.update(new UpdateWalletLimitsCommand(
                UUID.fromString(jwt.getSubject()),
                request.transferPerOperation(),
                request.transferDaily(),
                request.transferMonthly(),
                request.transferNightPerOperation(),
                request.transferNightWindow()
        ));
        return ResponseEntity.ok(toResponse(result));
    }

    private static WalletLimitsResponseDTO toResponse(WalletLimitsResult result) {
        return new WalletLimitsResponseDTO(
                new WalletLimitsResponseDTO.TransferLimits(
                        limitValue(result.transfer().perOperation()),
                        periodValue(result.transfer().daily()),
                        periodValue(result.transfer().monthly()),
                        limitValue(result.transfer().nightPerOperation()),
                        periodValue(result.transfer().nightWindow())
                ),
                new WalletLimitsResponseDTO.DepositLimits(
                        limitValue(result.deposit().perOperation()),
                        periodValue(result.deposit().daily())
                ),
                new WalletLimitsResponseDTO.Night(
                        result.night().start(),
                        result.night().end(),
                        result.night().timezone(),
                        result.night().active()
                )
        );
    }

    private static WalletLimitsResponseDTO.LimitValue limitValue(WalletLimitsResult.LimitValue value) {
        return new WalletLimitsResponseDTO.LimitValue(value.effective(), value.global(), value.custom());
    }

    private static WalletLimitsResponseDTO.PeriodLimitValue periodValue(WalletLimitsResult.PeriodLimitValue value) {
        return new WalletLimitsResponseDTO.PeriodLimitValue(
                value.effective(),
                value.global(),
                value.custom(),
                value.used(),
                value.available()
        );
    }
```

`src/test/java/ElFabrica/Wallet_pay/support/TestCpf.java`:

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support;

import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;

public final class TestCpf {

    private TestCpf() {
    }

    public static String generate() {
        int[] digits = new int[11];
        ThreadLocalRandom random = ThreadLocalRandom.current();
        do {
            for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
                digits[i] = random.nextInt(10);
            }
        } while (allEqual(digits));
        digits[9] = checkDigit(digits, 9);
        digits[10] = checkDigit(digits, 10);

        StringBuilder cpf = new StringBuilder();
        for (int digit : digits) {
            cpf.append(digit);
        }
        return cpf.toString();
    }

    private static int checkDigit(int[] digits, int length) {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            sum += digits[i] * (length + 1 - i);
        }
        int remainder = (sum * 10) % 11;
        return remainder == 10 ? 0 : remainder;
    }

    private static boolean allEqual(int[] digits) {
        for (int i = 1; i < 9; i++) {
            if (digits[i] != digits[0]) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}
```

```java
package ElFabrica.Wallet_pay;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.AbstractIntegrationTest;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.CapturingEmailVerificationSender;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestCpf;
import ElFabrica.Wallet_pay.support.TestUser;
import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MvcResult;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockHttpServletRequestBuilder;

class FullFlowIntegrationTest extends AbstractIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private CapturingEmailVerificationSender emailVerificationSender;

    private final List<MvcResult> responses = new ArrayList<>();

    @Test
    void shouldRunTheCompleteFinancialFlow() throws Exception {
        Session joao = registerVerifyAndLogin("Joao Silva", "joao.e2e@email.com");
        Session maria = registerVerifyAndLogin("Maria Souza", "maria.e2e@email.com");

        call(post("/wallets/me/deposits")
                .header("Authorization", joao.bearer())
                .header("Idempotency-Key", "e2e-deposito-1")
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "amount": "500.00"
                        }
                        """), 201);
        MvcResult transfer = call(post("/transactions/transfers")
                .header("Authorization", joao.bearer())
                .header("Idempotency-Key", "e2e-transferencia-1")
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "receiverUserId": "%s",
                          "amount": "120.00",
                          "description": "Aluguel"
                        }
                        """.formatted(maria.userId())), 201);
        String transferId = read(transfer, "$.transactionId");

        assertThat(read(call(get("/wallets/me/balance").header("Authorization", joao.bearer()), 200), "$.balance"))
                .isEqualTo("380.00");
        assertThat(read(call(get("/wallets/me/balance").header("Authorization", maria.bearer()), 200), "$.balance"))
                .isEqualTo("120.00");

        String joaoStatement = body(call(get("/wallets/me/statement").header("Authorization", joao.bearer()), 200));
        List<String> joaoDepositDirections = JsonPath.read(joaoStatement, "$.items[?(@.type == 'DEPOSIT')].direction");
        List<String> joaoTransferDirections = JsonPath.read(joaoStatement, "$.items[?(@.type == 'TRANSFER')].direction");
        List<String> joaoCounterparties = JsonPath.read(joaoStatement, "$.items[?(@.type == 'TRANSFER')].counterpartyName");
        assertThat(joaoDepositDirections).containsExactly("IN");
        assertThat(joaoTransferDirections).containsExactly("OUT");
        assertThat(joaoCounterparties).containsExactly("Maria Souza");

        String mariaStatement = body(call(get("/wallets/me/statement").header("Authorization", maria.bearer()), 200));
        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(mariaStatement, "$.totalElements")).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat((String) JsonPath.read(mariaStatement, "$.items[0].direction")).isEqualTo("IN");
        assertThat((String) JsonPath.read(mariaStatement, "$.items[0].counterpartyName")).isEqualTo("Joao Silva");

        MvcResult receipt = call(get("/transactions/{id}/receipt", transferId).header("Authorization", maria.bearer()), 200);
        assertThat(read(receipt, "$.amount")).isEqualTo("120.00");
        assertThat(read(receipt, "$.senderName")).isEqualTo("Joao Silva");

        assertThat(auditActionCounts())
                .containsEntry("USER_REGISTERED", 2L)
                .containsEntry("EMAIL_VERIFIED", 2L)
                .containsEntry("LOGIN_SUCCEEDED", 2L)
                .containsEntry("DEPOSIT_CREATED", 1L)
                .containsEntry("TRANSFER_CREATED", 1L);

        UUID joaoWalletId = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select id from wallets where user_id = ?", UUID.class, joao.userId()
        );
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select count(*) from transactions_aud where id = ?", Long.class, UUID.fromString(transferId)
        )).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select count(*) from wallets_aud where id = ?", Long.class, joaoWalletId
        )).isEqualTo(3L);

        assertThat(responses).isNotEmpty().allSatisfy(response ->
                assertThat(response.getResponse().getHeader("X-Request-Id")).isNotBlank());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldReturnUniformErrorsOnFailurePaths() throws Exception {
        TestUser joao = createVerifiedUser("Joao Silva", "100.00");
        TestUser maria = createVerifiedUser("Maria Souza", "0.00");
        TestUser rico = createVerifiedUser("Rico Souza", "6000.00");

        assertError(call(get("/wallets/me/balance"), 401), "UNAUTHORIZED");

        assertError(call(transferRequest(joao, maria, "150.00", "e2e-erro-1"), 422), "INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE");
        assertThat(jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
                "select failure_code from audit_events where action = 'TRANSFER_REJECTED'", String.class
        )).isEqualTo("INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE");

        assertError(call(transferRequest(rico, maria, "5000.01", "e2e-erro-2"), 422), "LIMIT_EXCEEDED");

        call(transferRequest(joao, maria, "10.00", "e2e-erro-3"), 201);
        assertError(call(transferRequest(joao, maria, "20.00", "e2e-erro-3"), 422), "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED");

        assertError(call(post("/transactions/transfers")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(joao))
                .header("Idempotency-Key", "e2e-erro-4")
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("{"), 400), "MALFORMED_REQUEST");

        assertError(call(get("/rota-inexistente").header("Authorization", bearer(joao)), 404), "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND");

        MvcResult unexpected = call(get("/test-support/boom").header("Authorization", bearer(joao)), 500);
        assertError(unexpected, "INTERNAL_ERROR");
        assertThat(body(unexpected))
                .doesNotContain("detalhe interno secreto")
                .doesNotContain("IllegalStateException");
    }

    private Session registerVerifyAndLogin(String name, String email) throws Exception {
        MvcResult created = call(post("/users")
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "name": "%s",
                          "email": "%s",
                          "password": "%s",
                          "document": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(name, email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD, TestCpf.generate())), 201);
        UUID userId = UUID.fromString(read(created, "$.id"));

        call(post("/auth/email-verification/confirm")
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "token": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(emailVerificationSender.lastTokenFor(email))), 204);

        MvcResult login = call(post("/auth/login")
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "email": "%s",
                          "password": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD)), 200);
        return new Session(userId, read(login, "$.accessToken"));
    }

    private MockHttpServletRequestBuilder transferRequest(TestUser sender, TestUser receiver, String amount, String key) {
        return post("/transactions/transfers")
                .header("Authorization", bearer(sender))
                .header("Idempotency-Key", key)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("""
                        {
                          "receiverUserId": "%s",
                          "amount": "%s"
                        }
                        """.formatted(receiver.userId(), amount));
    }

    private MvcResult call(MockHttpServletRequestBuilder request, int expectedStatus) throws Exception {
        MvcResult result = mockMvc.perform(request).andExpect(status().is(expectedStatus)).andReturn();
        responses.add(result);
        return result;
    }

    private void assertError(MvcResult result, String expectedCode) throws Exception {
        String body = body(result);
        String requestId = result.getResponse().getHeader("X-Request-Id");

        assertThat((String) JsonPath.read(body, "$.code")).isEqualTo(expectedCode);
        assertThat((Integer) JsonPath.read(body, "$.status")).isEqualTo(result.getResponse().getStatus());
        assertThat((Map<String, Object>) JsonPath.read(body, "$.fields")).isNotNull();
        assertThat(requestId).isNotBlank();
        assertThat((String) JsonPath.read(body, "$.requestId")).isEqualTo(requestId);
    }

    private Map<String, Long> auditActionCounts() {
        return jdbcTemplate.query("select action, count(*) as total from audit_events group by action", resultSet -> {
            Map<String, Long> counts = new HashMap<>();
            while (resultSet.next()) {
                counts.put(resultSet.getString("action"), resultSet.getLong("total"));
            }
            return counts;
        });
    }

    private static String body(MvcResult result) throws Exception {
        return result.getResponse().getContentAsString();
    }

    private static String read(MvcResult result, String path) throws Exception {
        return JsonPath.read(body(result), path);
    }

    private record Session(UUID userId, String accessToken) {

        String bearer() {
            return "Bearer " + accessToken;
        }
    }
}
```

Se o compilador avisar de cast nao verificado em `(Map<String, Object>) JsonPath.read(...)`, anotar `assertError` com `@SuppressWarnings("unchecked")`.

O fluxo usa o relogio real; todos os valores ficam abaixo dos limites noturnos, entao o teste passa a qualquer hora.

- [ ] **Step 2: Rodar o teste ponta a ponta**

```bash
./mvnw test -Dtest=FullFlowIntegrationTest
```

Esperado: `Tests run: 2, Failures: 0, Errors: 0`. Se algum caminho falhar, o defeito esta na task que entregou aquele comportamento; corrigir la, nao relaxar o teste.

- [ ] **Step 3: Rodar a suite inteira**

```bash
./mvnw test
```

Esperado: `BUILD SUCCESS`, `Failures: 0, Errors: 0`.

```bash
git add -A src
```
